**[00:06:29.550] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000064)  
Good morning.

**[00:06:31.190] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000065)  
I again have the honor of welcoming our friends

**[00:06:37.090] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000066)  
from the D.C.

**[00:06:37.790] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000067)  
for the Department of Energy and the ERC

**[00:06:41.270] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000068)  
and the entire energy family

**[00:06:43.290] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000069)  
for the briefing of their proposed budget for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:06:50.190] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000070)  
153 pesos and 70 centavos.

**[00:06:54.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000071)  
This was the highest price of diesel

**[00:06:57.550] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000072)  
when the conflict between the United States and Iran

**[00:07:00.450] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000073)  
caused the effective closure of the Strait of Hormuz.

**[00:07:05.010] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000074)  
Transport operations,

**[00:07:07.230] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000075)  
food production,

**[00:07:07.790] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000076)  
businesses and households' budgets came under severe strain.

**[00:07:12.790] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000077)  
Deadline inflation reached 7.2% in April 2026,

**[00:07:18.790] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000078)  
while grid alerts heightened concerns over power reliability.

**[00:07:23.790] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000079)  
The President responded by declaring a State of National Energy Emergency

**[00:07:28.790] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000080)  
under Executive Order No. 110.

**[00:07:31.790] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000081)  
The order recognized what the crisis meant for us,

**[00:07:35.790] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000082)  
geopolitical countries,

**[00:07:37.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000083)  
political conflict had disrupted global energy supply chains

**[00:07:41.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000084)  
and placed upward pressure on fuel prices,

**[00:07:45.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000085)  
threatening the adequacy and stability of our domestic energy supply.

**[00:07:52.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000086)  
But while a declaration and emergency procurement can stabilize a crisis,

**[00:07:57.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000087)  
they cannot substitute for preparedness built before the shock arrives.

**[00:08:02.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000088)  
Leadership, competence and strong institutions matter.

**[00:08:06.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000089)  
That is why the Energy Family deserves both our support and our careful scrutiny.

**[00:08:13.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000090)  
Under the able leadership of the President,

**[00:08:16.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000091)  
the Uplift Committee,

**[00:08:18.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000092)  
and particularly through the efforts of our good Secretary Sharon Garin,

**[00:08:24.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000093)  
the Energy Family acted swiftly to stabilize supply

**[00:08:28.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000094)  
and limit further the domestic disruption.

**[00:08:31.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000095)  
These efforts deserve recognition,

**[00:08:34.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000096)  
even as they require our support.

**[00:08:36.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000097)  
It is time for us to ask why extraordinary emergency measures became necessary.

**[00:08:42.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000098)  
DOE acted immediately,

**[00:08:44.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000099)  
conserving critical inventories,

**[00:08:47.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000100)  
improving fuel dispatch,

**[00:08:49.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000101)  
securing more than 178 million liters of diesel to build an emergency fuel buffer,

**[00:08:56.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000102)  
managing price adjustments,

**[00:08:58.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000103)  
supporting targeted assistance,

**[00:09:01.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000104)  
guarding against hoarding and profiteering,

**[00:09:04.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000105)  
and strengthening conservation measures.

**[00:09:07.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000106)  
This was a significant corrective measure,

**[00:09:10.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000107)  
but it must now be converted into a permanent,

**[00:09:14.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000108)  
rules-based preparedness mechanism.

**[00:09:17.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000109)  
The ERC intensified market oversight and adopted measures

**[00:09:22.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000110)  
to caution consumers from abrupt fuel shocks.

**[00:09:26.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000111)  
Its heightened data requirements helped identify generation costs,

**[00:09:30.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000112)  
pressures and the interventions needed.

**[00:09:33.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000113)  
For fiscal year 2027,

**[00:09:36.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000114)  
this capacity should be strengthened through real-time monitoring,

**[00:09:41.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000115)  
faster regulatory action,

**[00:09:43.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000116)  
and automatic protection for qualified lifeline consumers.

**[00:09:47.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000117)  
The Energy Family stood on the front line of the response

**[00:09:51.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000118)  
and reduced the risk of a deeper disruption.

**[00:09:54.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000119)  
At the same time, the crisis revealed an implementation gap.

**[00:09:58.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000120)  
A strategic petroleum reserve had been established in policy,

**[00:10:02.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000121)  
since 2021,

**[00:10:04.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000122)  
and supported by a completed study in 2022.

**[00:10:08.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000123)  
Although it was not fully operational when the shock arrived,

**[00:10:12.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000124)  
the lesson is clear.

**[00:10:14.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000125)  
Policy declarations must become funded,

**[00:10:18.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000126)  
measurable and operational capabilities.

**[00:10:23.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000127)  
In a State of the Nation address,

**[00:10:25.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000128)  
the President spoke at length about the energy industry.

**[00:10:29.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000129)  
He aptly said,

**[00:10:30.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000130)  
the past few months have reminded us that we need to increase

**[00:10:35.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000131)  
the amount of electricity we are receiving.

**[00:10:41.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000132)  
This is a sign of a strong country.

**[00:10:45.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000133)  
The President called for stronger energy security,

**[00:10:50.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000134)  
improved investment conditions for local and foreign capital,

**[00:10:54.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000135)  
and an expedited shift toward indigenous renewable energy.

**[00:10:58.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000136)  
Together with energy storage and more reliable transmission.

**[00:11:07.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000137)  
His emphasis on these priorities reflects the central role of the Energy Family

**[00:11:13.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000138)  
in advancing his vision of a stronger,

**[00:11:16.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000139)  
more resilient and more prosperous Philippines.

**[00:11:20.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000140)  
To my fellow legislators,

**[00:11:22.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000141)  
a country that needs energy security cannot afford,

**[00:11:26.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000142)  
and under an unprecedented crisis,

**[00:11:27.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000143)  
an underfunded energy sector.

**[00:11:30.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000144)  
We have just witnessed what a war happening thousands of miles away

**[00:11:35.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000145)  
can do to our energy supply.

**[00:11:37.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000146)  
And we must now prepare for El Nino,

**[00:11:40.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000147)  
related risk to hydropower, peak demand and reserve margins.

**[00:11:45.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000148)  
But we have also seen what the Energy Family can accomplish

**[00:11:50.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000149)  
when institutions act decisively.

**[00:11:53.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000150)  
The challenge now is to institutionalize the capacity

**[00:11:56.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000151)  
before the next emergency.

**[00:11:58.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000152)  
Swift action matters.

**[00:12:00.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000153)  
Intelligent policy interventions matter.

**[00:12:04.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000154)  
Regulatory capacity, technical capacity,

**[00:12:07.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000155)  
and consumer protection all matter.

**[00:12:10.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000156)  
And all of these require sustained,

**[00:12:13.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000157)  
well-targeted public support backed by measurable performance.

**[00:12:18.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000158)  
The combined proposal budget of the Energy Family

**[00:12:22.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000159)  
in the amount of 56.1 billion

**[00:12:25.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000160)  
is not simply an expenditure.

**[00:12:28.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000161)  
It is an investment in national resilience,

**[00:12:32.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000162)  
an insurance policy against disruption,

**[00:12:35.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000163)  
and a catalyst for the reliable, affordable,

**[00:12:38.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000164)  
and secure energy system our economy requires.

**[00:12:42.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000165)  
The budget should specify targets for strategic fuel reserves

**[00:12:46.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000166)  
and days of supply,

**[00:12:49.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000167)  
diversified import sources,

**[00:12:52.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000168)  
emergency procurement lead times,

**[00:12:54.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000169)  
lead reserve margins,

**[00:12:56.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000170)  
transmission and storage completion,

**[00:12:59.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000171)  
off-grid fuel security,

**[00:13:01.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000172)  
renewable capacity,

**[00:13:03.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000173)  
and consumer protection.

**[00:13:05.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000174)  
Let us provide the Energy Family with the resources

**[00:13:09.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000175)  
and the laws it needs,

**[00:13:11.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000176)  
not only to respond to the next crisis,

**[00:13:14.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000177)

**[00:15:48.960] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000225)  
good morning allow me to present the department of energy's major accomplishment

**[00:15:54.240] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000226)

**[00:16:00.320] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000227)  
year allow me to begin with the department's major accomplishments in response to the energy

**[00:16:07.040] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000228)  
emergency caused by the middle east conflict the doe in coordination with other agencies

**[00:16:14.320] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000229)  
employed a whole of government approach in securing fuel supply implementing staggered

**[00:16:20.480] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000230)  
fuel price adjustments prescribing the price adjustment

**[00:16:23.600] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000231)  
for oil companies implementing fuel subsidy programs preventing hoarding and profiteering

**[00:16:30.240] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000232)  
enforcing energy efficiency and conservation measures and setting up support measures

**[00:16:35.520] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000233)  
for the transport and agricultural sectors the country maintains an average available fuel supply

**[00:16:42.640] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000234)  
of 50 days following the president's directive during the 2025 sauna the doe with nea and erc

**[00:16:52.320] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000235)  
restored

**[00:16:53.600] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000236)  
24-7 electricity service in Siquijor. The department also caused decisively to address

**[00:17:00.140] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000237)  
the prolonged power interruptions in Catanduanas, working closely with NPC, NEA, and FISELCO

**[00:17:06.980] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000238)  
to restore reliable electricity service across the province. The department has also been

**[00:17:13.880] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000239)  
unwavering in its disaster response efforts to ensure power restoration and continuity

**[00:17:20.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000240)  
of fuel supply in affected areas. As of December 2025, the country's household

**[00:17:27.420] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000241)  
electrification level has reached 94.93%, with around 28.75 million households energized

**[00:17:36.280] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000242)  
out of the 30.28 million potential households. Our RE, or renewable energy share, in the

**[00:17:45.380] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000243)  
generation mix increased to 25.04% in 2025.

**[00:17:50.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000244)  
Through the Green Energy Auction Program, we have already awarded 20.7 GW of RE capacity,

**[00:18:04.520] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000245)  
with another 25 GW planned for auction from 2027 to 2035.

**[00:18:11.740] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000246)  
The RE sector continues to be the leading contributor to Green Lane investments, with

**[00:18:18.420] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000247)  
187 endorsed projects, amounting to 5.46 trillion pesos since 2023, accounting for 86% of investments

**[00:18:20.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000248)  
projects, amounting to 5.46 trillion pesos since 2023, accounting for 86% of investments

**[00:18:31.000] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000249)  
under the Board of Investments' Green Lane program.

**[00:18:35.740] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000250)  
From the start of the Marcos Jr.'s administration to date, the country's power capacity continued

**[00:18:42.540] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000251)  
to grow, with installed capacity increasing by 5,461

**[00:18:48.560] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000252)  
megawatts, or 21.1%

**[00:18:50.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000253)  
increase, while dependable capacity increased by 5,400

**[00:18:56.080] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000254)  
megawatts, or a 23.5% increase. To further support the timely delivery of committed

**[00:19:03.960] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000255)  
power projects and ensure that capacity additions are brought online as scheduled, the DOE established

**[00:19:12.140] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000256)  
Task Force 200, pursuant to the President's pronouncements during the 2025 SONA. The Task

**[00:19:19.880] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000257)  
Force

**[00:19:20.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000258)  
closely monitors and

**[00:19:21.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000259)  
facilitates the implementation

**[00:19:22.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000260)  
of 200 committed

**[00:19:23.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000261)  
projects. To date,

**[00:19:24.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000262)  
48 power projects

**[00:19:25.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000263)  
are now completed and

**[00:19:26.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000264)  
commercially operational,

**[00:19:27.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000265)  
adding 1,846.63 MW to the grid.

**[00:19:28.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000266)  
provide the power to the entire power sector, and which will improve the power and utility

**[00:19:29.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000267)  
efficiency of the power grid in the future.

**[00:19:30.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000268)  
The DOE project is also an open and sustainable project with the goal to increase the full

**[00:19:31.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000269)  
power generation capacity, which takes place in 2020 by Delegation of the Power Project

**[00:19:32.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000270)  
and the immediate development of best power generation system.

**[00:19:33.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000271)  
The DOE project is expected to receive a total of 4, constructive design from KMT, and will

**[00:19:34.440] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000272)  
46.63 megawatts to the grid. This includes 12 BESS projects with a combined capacity

**[00:19:42.200] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000273)  
of 229.37 megawatts that support grid stability and frequency regulation.

**[00:19:50.660] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000274)  
In the upstream sector, the 14 petroleum service contracts recently signed by the President

**[00:19:56.760] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000275)  
will generate $324 million dollars potential investments.

**[00:20:03.160] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000276)  
The successful drilling of Camago 3 and Malampaya East 1 wells under Service Contract 38 yielded

**[00:20:10.640] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000277)  
estimated reserves of 222 billion cubic feet of gas, extending operations to 2034 and providing

**[00:20:19.680] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000278)

**[00:20:26.280] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000279)

**[00:20:26.740] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000280)  
We present the Department's proposed 2027 budget.

**[00:20:31.560] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000281)  
The DOE's revenue collections for the National Government have consistently exceeded its

**[00:20:38.020] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000282)  
approved budget. Collections were driven mainly by the Malampaya

**[00:20:42.640] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000283)  
Gas Fund. Its decline, however, was offset by improved collections from RE sources.

**[00:20:49.700] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000284)  
For 2026, the total projected revenue collection is 6.06 billion pesos.

**[00:20:56.260] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000285)  
For 2027, revenues are projected to reach 23 billion, mainly because of Malampaya Phase

**[00:21:04.000] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000286)  
IV. RE collections are also expected to continue increasing.

**[00:21:07.540] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000287)  
For fiscal year 2027, the DOE's total proposed budget is 5.58 billion, reflecting an increase

**[00:21:14.320] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000288)  
of 1.14 billion, or 25.74%, compared with the fiscal year 2026 GAA of

**[00:21:20.320] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000289)  
4.43 billion pesos.

**[00:21:25.660] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000290)  
The 2027 budget is composed of 36.4% new appropriations and 63.6% automatic

**[00:21:37.740] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000291)  
appropriations from the special account in the General Fund. In other words, the Department's

**[00:21:43.660] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000292)  
budgetary requirements will be heavily supported through the SAGF. This chart shows the DOE's

**[00:21:51.980] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000293)  
budget utilization rate from 2022 to 31 July 2026. As of 31 August 2026, the obligation rate is at 49%.

**[00:22:03.660] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000294)  
Upon completion of the procurement of the mobile energy system and the DOE automation project,

**[00:22:10.940] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000295)  
which are already undergoing procurement, the obligation rate will increase to 68.31%.

**[00:22:17.740] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000296)  
The DOE expects to reach at least 95.8%

**[00:22:21.980] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000297)  
utilization rate for 2026. The increase in the DOE's recommended 2027 budget from the current

**[00:22:30.620] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000298)  
year's budget level is in large part owing to increased funding for these three major projects.

**[00:22:37.580] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000299)  
The Philippine Grade Geometry and Seismic Survey Project, or simply the Grade Geometry Project.

**[00:22:43.580] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000300)  
Second, the Mobile Energy Systems, or the MES, under the Total Electrification Project. And third,

**[00:22:51.420] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000301)  
the issp which includes the doe automation project the gray geometry project is the

**[00:22:58.140] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000302)  
department's initiative to improve resource mapping and prospect identification reduce

**[00:23:04.940] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000303)  
exploration risk and help attract greater upstream energy investments in the philippines

**[00:23:10.300] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000304)  
by generating higher quality geophysical data across priority petroleum basins the

**[00:23:16.460] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000305)  
grid geometry project covers 120 000 square kilometers the area would support up to 120

**[00:23:24.220] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000306)  
individual exploration service contracts using the data from the 12 recently awarded exploration

**[00:23:31.260] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000307)  
contracts representing just 10 percent of the total potential blocks 12 contracts could already

**[00:23:38.540] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000308)  
account for approximately 16.8 billion pesos of potential private sector investments and that

**[00:23:45.980] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000309)  
doesn't

**[00:23:46.460] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000310)  
even include actual discoveries under the total electrification project the department is

**[00:23:53.500] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000311)  
procuring mes that will provide renewable rapidly deployable and modular solutions

**[00:24:01.900] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000312)  
designed for immediate interim and sustainable electricity access for both disaster response

**[00:24:09.420] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000313)  
and electrification purposes for 2026 we are utilizing 500 million from current

**[00:24:15.980] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000314)  
appropriations and 21 million from continuing appropriations for 2027 we are proposing 600

**[00:24:23.820] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000315)  
million budget for at least 60 additional mes units for 2027 we propose a budget of 950 million

**[00:24:33.260] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000316)  
for issp of which 491 million or 52 is for automation while 458 million or 48 is for other ict requirements

**[00:24:45.980] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000317)  
the automation project will transform fragmented standalone systems into a unified

**[00:24:52.780] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000318)  
streamlined automated and ai enabled enterprise platform it will improve efficiency and

**[00:24:59.260] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000319)  
transparency strengthen accountability and regular regulatory compliance and reduce opportunity

**[00:25:05.740] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000320)  
opportunities for discretion manipulation and corruption finally we present a summary of the

**[00:25:12.940] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000321)  
department's major plans and programs

**[00:25:16.220] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000322)  
first we accelerate energy resource development the biggest investment of the doe is on the

**[00:25:22.380] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000323)  
grid geometry project which is expected to significantly increase oil and gas exploration

**[00:25:29.340] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000324)  
in the country we are implementing the philippine geothermal de-risking facility to revive geothermal

**[00:25:36.460] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000325)  
exploration in the philippines we are also conducting additional green energy auctions such

**[00:25:42.940] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000326)  
as waste-to-energy, offshore wind, agro-solar, solar on stilts, solar on irrigation canals,

**[00:25:50.060] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000327)  
and off-grid areas. Nuclear energy remains part of the DOE's long-term energy strategy,

**[00:25:57.340] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000328)  
functioning as cheaper and more reliable baseload power. The mid-merit natural gas capacity auction

**[00:26:04.620] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000329)  
is targeted to be completed by December of 2026. We also have major plans and programs

**[00:26:12.300] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000330)  
to ensure energy security, including the deployment of MES, establishment of a state-owned

**[00:26:19.500] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000331)  
strategic petroleum reserve, permutation of the Visayas, Mindoro, and Palawan energy master

**[00:26:26.380] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000332)  
plants, implementation of policies for generation companies' accountability, review of systems loss

**[00:26:34.620] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000333)  
related policies and programs, push for government-wide adoption of electric vehicles or EVs,

**[00:26:42.300] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000334)  
and deployment of EV charging stations. We also have several key projects to strengthen

**[00:26:48.780] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000335)  
governance, including the full digitalization, automation, and AI transformation of DOE,

**[00:26:56.860] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000336)  
the establishment of the Center for Energy Excellence, comprising of the Energy Museum,

**[00:27:02.860] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000337)  
Energy Academy, and the R&D Center, the strengthening of DOE manpower, the modernization

**[00:27:12.300] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000338)  
nationwide command center, and the upgrading of DOE facilities to achieve net-zero energy consumption.

**[00:27:21.100] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000339)  
Mr. Chair, the honorable members of the committee, this ends my presentation.

**[00:27:26.220] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000340)  
Thank you for your kind attention.

**[00:27:34.590] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000341)  
Thank you, Yusek. Thank you very much. Next to present, Chairman Francis Wan. You have the floor, sir.

**[00:27:47.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000342)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chairperson and the honorable members of the committee.

**[00:27:56.140] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000343)  
Good morning. I am Francis Attenino C. Wan, the chairperson and CEO of the Energy Regulatory

**[00:28:03.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000344)  
Commission, and thank you for this opportunity to present our budget for 2027.

**[00:28:12.140] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000345)  
The ERC is an independent quasi-judicial body created under the IPERA.

**[00:28:16.620] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000346)  
And with me in the commission are four other commissioners, Commissioner Florescinda Digal, Marco Fuentes, Amante Liberato, and Paris Real.

**[00:28:28.300] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000347)  
Our mandate is to promote market competition, ensure consumer choice, protect consumer interests, keep retail rates based on prudent and reasonable cost recovery, and penalize abuse of market power.

**[00:28:44.840] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000348)  
We regulate and oversee the full electricity value chain, the regulated sectors, namely the transmission and the distribution sectors, as well as the competitive sectors, the generation and supply.

**[00:28:59.800] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000349)  
And at the center of everything we do are the consumers, upholding the rights and interests of our consumers, both captive and contestable.

**[00:29:10.180] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000350)  
The slide four is on the...

**[00:29:16.570] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000351)  
In the sector that we regulate, it is currently valued at around 4.19 trillion.

**[00:29:24.510] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000352)  
And this is just to emphasize the scale of the sector that our proposed budget of 944 million must support.

**[00:29:35.270] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000353)  
For ERC accomplishments, we have issued so far for a period from January 2025 until June 2026,

**[00:29:45.330] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000354)  
4,000...

**[00:29:46.570] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000355)  
and 611 decisions and orders.

**[00:29:50.470] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000356)  
We have imposed 157 millions in fines.

**[00:29:55.210] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000357)  
We have directed the refund of around 71.3 billion.

**[00:30:00.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000358)  
P70 billion of that came from Meralco's filing of its actual weighted average tariff for the lapsed years during the time when its rates were not reset by the earlier commission.

**[00:30:16.160] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000359)  
We have approved 292 power supply agreements.

**[00:30:21.340] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000360)  
These are the agreements between distribution utilities and generation companies for the supply of electricity.

**[00:30:27.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000361)  
And 184 of that with the final authority.

**[00:30:32.800] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000362)  
Comparing that to the performance of ERC during the entire 2024, we have approved in 2024 only two power supply agreement applications and five CAPEX applications.

**[00:30:52.300] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000363)  
For the lifeline rate program in...

**[00:30:57.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000364)  
response to the call of the president to expand the scope and coverage of the lifeline program under IPIRA as extended by law.

**[00:31:11.240] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000365)  
We have actually launched a national lifeline program, expanded its coverage to up to 50 kilowatt hours for the marginalized end users.

**[00:31:25.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000366)  
And right now, the coverage...

**[00:31:27.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000367)  
is up by 85.33%.

**[00:31:31.540] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000368)  
And right now, we are able to cater to around 620,000 qualified marginalized end users.

**[00:31:40.500] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000369)  
Net metering also, in response to the call of the president, net metering registrations are up 51.77%.

**[00:31:49.520] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000370)  
And so far as the implementation of retail competition or ARCOA, with even the lowering of the threshold...

**[00:31:57.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000371)  
to 100 kilowatt, where those with demand of 100 kilowatt can now choose their own supplier, we've seen and estimated that the cost savings to these customers that opted to avail of their choice and contracted with retailers, the estimated cost savings to them already amounted to 19.87 billion.

**[00:32:24.960] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000372)  
So, among the landmark...

**[00:32:27.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000373)  
decisions that we have issued, aside from the lowering of the contestable threshold to 100 kilowatt, we have issued the first ever transmission wheeling rules for the off-grid.

**[00:32:39.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000374)  
This is the first time that we would have a regulatory framework for the transmission service to be provided in the off-grid areas, starting with Mindoro and Palawan as soon as this is established.

**[00:32:56.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000375)  
We have...

**[00:32:57.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000376)  
restarted the rate reset of our private distribution utilities and directed the private distribution utilities to respectively file their actual weighted average tariff filings.

**[00:33:14.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000377)  
And we have also addressed thousands of CAPEX filings and point-to-point transmission proposals.

**[00:33:27.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000378)  
We have disposed of thousands of them.

**[00:33:31.260] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000379)  
And we have supported the push for renewable energy by performing our part in the J-UP program of the Department of Energy.

**[00:33:48.960] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000380)  
We have set the reserve price for the off-grid.

**[00:33:53.460] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000381)  
For the offshore wind, we have also...

**[00:33:57.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000382)  
set the reserve price for the Pioneer Waste to Energy Projects.

**[00:34:03.220] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000383)  
We have established for the first time the Green Energy Auction Allowance to be able to support the new capacities that are being built under the J-UP program of the Department of Energy.

**[00:34:21.170] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000384)  
We have also updated our rules on the advanced metering infrastructure...

**[00:34:29.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000385)  
so that we can finally modernize our grid.

**[00:34:34.910] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000386)  
We have addressed long-standing issues in the generation sector, such as the public offering requirement,

**[00:34:41.990] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000387)  
the fit adjustment issue.

**[00:34:44.630] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000388)  
We have updated the fit allowance.

**[00:34:47.610] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000389)  
We have updated the UCME as well as the SAGAR in some of the off-grid areas.

**[00:34:55.230] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000390)  
And every one of these, among others, was delivered within...

**[00:34:59.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000391)  
our approved budget for 2026.

**[00:35:03.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000392)  
And for 2027, our priorities remain to have fair and evidence-based rate setting, timely resolution of pending cases, robust oversight to foster competition, and a stable and enhanced regulatory environment to attract investment in our sector.

**[00:35:25.170] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000393)  
And all these are anchored in proactive consumer finance.

**[00:35:29.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000394)  
For our thrust on fair and evidence-based rate setting, aside from setting the gear prices for our J-UP, we have already issued the draft rules governing the regulatory framework for our gas auction.

**[00:35:53.170] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000395)  
We will soon be finalizing that.

**[00:35:56.290] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000396)  
We are targeting to...

**[00:35:59.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000397)  
enforce more strictly the distribution utilities' least-cost supply obligations under our revised 2026 CSP guidelines.

**[00:36:12.650] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000398)  
And soon, the guidelines that we will issue on the review of the terms of reference to be used by our distribution utilities that will be conducting their CSPs.

**[00:36:24.770] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000399)  
And that will also include our target of conducting more timely...

**[00:36:29.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000400)  
audits of the de-use implementation of their pass-through charges.

**[00:36:33.350] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000401)  
We will also complete the reset of the rates of the distribution utilities, both the private distribution utilities and the electric cooperatives.

**[00:36:47.330] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000402)  
This has not been done in more than 10 years.

**[00:36:50.910] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000403)  
There has been no new rates approved for our distribution utilities.

**[00:36:55.670] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000404)  
But only just last month.

**[00:36:59.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000405)  
We have already approved the final determination for the rate reset of one of our private distribution utilities belonging to the Group A under the performance-based regulation.

**[00:37:13.230] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000406)  
And all the other distribution utilities in Group A will follow suit.

**[00:37:17.870] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000407)  
We are targeting to complete the rate reset of Group B within the year and for Group C and D for the private distribution utilities.

**[00:37:29.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000408)  
All this will happen in 2027.

**[00:37:32.070] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000409)  
So this is something that was not done in more than a decade.

**[00:37:36.290] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000410)  
And we are targeting to finish the rate reset of the private distribution utilities in 2027.

**[00:37:44.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000411)  
Also, the new rates of our electric cooperatives, we target that they would have already their new rates to be implemented starting January.

**[00:37:55.770] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000412)  
Again, this is something that has not been done.

**[00:37:59.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000413)  
For more than a decade.

**[00:38:00.990] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000414)  
The last rate setting for our electric cooperatives happened in early 2010.

**[00:38:09.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000415)  
So after more than a decade, we will soon have their new rates.

**[00:38:14.670] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000416)  
And this is in time also for the push now for our distribution utilities to address their system losses.

**[00:38:24.690] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000417)  
And prepare them for the eventual removal of the system losses.

**[00:38:29.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000418)  
As indicated by the President.

**[00:38:35.370] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000419)  
For the trust on consumer-focused leadership, we have just approved the revised Magna Carta for electricity consumers.

**[00:38:47.110] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000420)  
And we intend to conduct an extensive information and education campaign across the country to be able to educate and inform.

**[00:39:00.590] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000421)  
Our consumers as regards their rights under the Magna Carta.

**[00:39:06.470] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000422)  
For our budget, our budget for 2027.

**[00:39:13.250] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000423)  
Or over the years, our GAA grew modestly from 911.6 million in 2024 to 931 million in 2026.

**[00:39:26.030] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000424)  
Or increases of roughly 2%.

**[00:39:29.190] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000425)  
And then.

**[00:39:30.590] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000426)  
0.04%.

**[00:39:32.150] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000427)  
We have proposed a budget of 1.7 billion to be able to cover all the plans and programs that we intend to implement for 2027.

**[00:39:47.270] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000428)  
Unfortunately, the NEP only reflects 944.065 million or 44.67% below what we proposed.

**[00:39:59.170] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000429)  
The variance.

**[00:40:00.590] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000430)  
Is.

**[00:40:02.150] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000431)  
Concentrated mainly in the MOOE and capital outlay.

**[00:40:06.690] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000432)  
And in the MOOE included there is the budget for the consultancy services that we intended supposedly to assist us complete the rate reset of our private distribution utilities and electric cooperatives as well as NGCP for 2027.

**[00:40:27.430] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000433)  
Our overall utilization rate has climbed.

**[00:40:30.590] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000434)  
Steadily.

**[00:40:31.190] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000435)  
So that will show our absorptive capacity and our ability to responsibly manage a larger budget.

**[00:40:43.490] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000436)  
So from a low of 28.08% in 2021, it has risen to 46.81 in June of 2026.

**[00:40:55.430] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000437)  
So that covers the first semester of the year.

**[00:40:59.090] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000438)  
And.

**[00:41:00.590] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000439)  
On an annual basis, our full year utilization last year was already at 84.20% up from a low of 62%.

**[00:41:13.490] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000440)  
In 2023, our revenues, we have consistently brought in more to the to the government in terms of the supervision fees, as well as the permit fees and fines that we collect.

**[00:41:30.590] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000441)  
And.

**[00:41:31.250] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000442)  
The.

**[00:41:31.550] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000443)  
And.

**[00:41:32.370] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000444)  
In 2025.

**[00:41:34.130] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000445)  
With a budget of 20 of 931 million, we were able to bring to the government a total of 2.354 billion in revenue or 121% above our, our projection.

**[00:41:52.710] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000446)  
And definitely way above the budget that was allocated for us in 2025.

**[00:41:59.210] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000447)  
And.

**[00:42:00.590] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000448)  
We expect that we will hit the same level for 2027 so bottom line is the ERC is a net revenue generator for the government and this budget request of 1.7 billion would have been offset by what we return to the treasury and unfortunately our NEP stands out.

**[00:42:30.590] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000449)  
It stands only at 944 million. That will be the end of my presentation. Your honors, thank you very much.

**[00:42:38.490] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000450)  
Thank you, Chair. This is going to be a working lunch. Para lang malaman ng colleagues natin, we distributed a presentation of NEA, NAPOCOR, PNOC, PNOC-EC, PISAM, and TRANSCO.

**[00:43:01.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000451)  
So nasa inyo na po yung briefer po nila. Para po mas mabilis po tayo.

**[00:43:08.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000452)  
But before I call on to the interpreter, I would like to recognize Congressman Salvador Plato, Vice Chair Bernadette Barberis, Vice Chair Maria Cristina Talavera Lopez, Deputy Minority Leader Presley Desus, Congressman John Chan,

**[00:43:31.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000453)  
Congressman Julio Cesar Vergara, Congressman Steve Solon, Congressman Dibutuan, Congressman Ernel Ceniza, Congressman Munir Arbizon, Congressman Joseph Yu, Vice Chair Isabela Zagadbaria, Representative Catherine Guraceta.

**[00:43:56.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000454)  
Thank you for your presence.

**[00:44:01.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000455)  
The interpellation may remind our distinguished colleagues that each member is allotted five minutes for questions or interpellation, excluding the response of the resource person.

**[00:44:17.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000456)  
When the bell sounds, the members have 30 minutes to conclude or wind up.

**[00:44:30.110] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000457)  
Consistent with the longstanding practice of the House, the Chair shall recognize minority.

**[00:44:35.410] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000458)  
Independent and majority members in an alternating manner when practicable.

**[00:44:41.550] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000459)  
The Chair first recognizes the Honorable Percy Zendanya from the minority.

**[00:44:48.590] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000460)  
Pag-uswang Percy, you have the floor.

**[00:44:50.730] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000461)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair. Magandang umaga po sa DOE family led by Secretary Sharon.

**[00:44:57.990] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000462)  
Ito po mga nakakaraang araw.

**[00:45:00.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000463)  
karami-rami na po tayo ng mga hinarap ng mga budget briefings.

**[00:45:03.400] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000464)  
At kadalasan po, yung mga budget briefings na ito ay mga grievance mechanisms

**[00:45:08.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000465)  
para po sa mga departamento na nakaranas ng matinding mga pagbabas sa kanilang budget.

**[00:45:14.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000466)  
At siguro po, in so many days, this is the first time na wala akong gano'ng narinig

**[00:45:18.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000467)  
na grievance from our colleagues from the executive.

**[00:45:22.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000468)  
Napansin ko rin po na across all areas, from PS, MOOE hanggang CO,

**[00:45:27.780] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000469)  
ay may increase po tayo with an overall increase of more than a little over 25%.

**[00:45:36.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000470)  
Secretary Green, why is this the case po for DOE kaya?

**[00:45:40.460] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000471)  
I think, Mr. Chair, I think because the increases are focused on three things.

**[00:45:49.260] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000472)  
It's the gradiometry, mobile energy system, and the automation.

**[00:45:53.700] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000473)  
Just briefly lang.

**[00:45:56.100] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000474)  
Yung gradiometry.

**[00:45:57.780] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000475)  
Kasi wala tayong data on what we have as resources here in the Philippines

**[00:46:02.460] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000476)  
on oil and gas and other matters.

**[00:46:04.700] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000477)  
So, this should have been done 10, 20 years back.

**[00:46:08.700] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000478)  
That's why we don't have any new exploration.

**[00:46:11.040] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000479)  
We only have malampaya until today.

**[00:46:13.500] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000480)  
So, with that new data, investors will see what we have.

**[00:46:17.440] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000481)  
We cannot sell something that we don't know what's in it.

**[00:46:19.980] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000482)  
So, that's why we need the gradiometry study.

**[00:46:22.820] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000483)  
It will take about 2 billion pesos.

**[00:46:25.580] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000484)  
This is a continuing project, 2026.

**[00:46:27.780] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000485)  
2026 was funded.

**[00:46:29.060] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000486)  
So, another budget was allotted for 2027, sir.

**[00:46:35.020] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000487)  
This is for energy security.

**[00:46:37.060] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000488)  
Now, the MES is a mobile energy system na parang siyang container.

**[00:46:41.200] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000489)  
Tapos may mga solar panels with battery and maybe turbina ng for diesel or whatever technology there is.

**[00:46:50.980] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000490)  
That will address that whenever there's an emergency,

**[00:46:53.680] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000491)  
we can deploy these units to, for example,

**[00:46:57.780] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000492)  
the usual path would be like Masbate, Bicol area, and up to the north.

**[00:47:04.740] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000493)  
So, we'll deploy it strategically in some parts also, in some islands na wala talagang connection.

**[00:47:11.280] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000494)  
We can put it there like what we did in Kalayaan.

**[00:47:15.380] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000495)  
Kalayaan ba tama?

**[00:47:16.540] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000496)  
Pag-asa pala.

**[00:47:17.820] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000497)  
Pag-asa.

**[00:47:19.180] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000498)  
The third one, sir, is the ISSP.

**[00:47:23.280] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000499)  
DOE needs to be automated.

**[00:47:24.820] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000500)  
Otherwise, there are more contracts.

**[00:47:27.780] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000501)  
Every year and every month, actually, but the system is still manual.

**[00:47:32.560] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000502)  
So, for us to have a more data-driven data analysis also of what we need as policies,

**[00:47:41.520] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000503)  
we need it to be automated so we can move faster and be more transparent.

**[00:47:47.280] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000504)  
So, this is to automate from front to back end, Mr. Chair.

**[00:47:51.960] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000505)  
So, those are the three that contributed to the 25% increase, Mr. Chair.

**[00:47:59.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000506)  
Kaya ako po tinanong, kasi mapapasana all po pala tayo when we listen to the budget of the DOE.

**[00:48:04.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000507)  
Sana yung equally critical agencies natin, lalong-lalong yung social services,

**[00:48:09.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000508)  
would also enjoy the same increase in their budget and not a significant cut as we have seen the past few days.

**[00:48:16.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000509)  
Thank you, Madam Secretary, for the very comprehensive explanation.

**[00:48:20.740] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000510)  
Alam ko po, bahagi ito na isang malaking programa at isang malaking plano.

**[00:48:24.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000511)  
Pero po para sa ating mga kababayan na nakikinig, given all this po,

**[00:48:28.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000512)  
bababa na po ba ang presyo ng kuryente?

**[00:48:29.480] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000513)  
Mr. Chair, just before that, Mr. Chair,

**[00:48:34.600] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000514)  
the 25% increase in comparison to the other agencies is 25% of 2.5 billion.

**[00:48:41.960] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000515)  
It's really very small compared to the other agencies.

**[00:48:45.340] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000516)  
Now, to lower the price of electricity, it's a very multifaceted issue.

**[00:48:51.620] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000517)  
We are, if we have, generally, yes, sir.

**[00:48:55.500] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000518)  
If you ask me, yes, it will lower the price.

**[00:48:58.000] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000519)  
If we have more oil and gas that are indigenous, yes, it will lower it.

**[00:49:01.880] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000520)  
If we have more renewable, yes, it will lower it.

**[00:49:05.320] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000521)  
If we have a good coordination between the policy and then the regulation,

**[00:49:12.300] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000522)  
those are several, we need several things to make it lower and lower.

**[00:49:17.080] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000523)  
And all these policies, eventually, the intention of all the policies that DOE is doing now,

**[00:49:23.360] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000524)  
the intention really is to lower the rates, sir.

**[00:49:26.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000525)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[00:49:28.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000526)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Secretary, for giving that assurance sa mga kababayan po nating nanonood.

**[00:49:33.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000527)  
At araw-araw po ay kinakabanting makakatanggap ng kanilang electric bill.

**[00:49:37.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000528)  
Alam na po siguro ng NEA na itatanong ko ang question na ito.

**[00:49:41.420] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000529)  
Sukin nyo na po ako taon-taon.

**[00:49:43.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000530)  
Pero nalulungkot po ako na sukin nyo ako taon-taon.

**[00:49:46.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000531)  
Last year po sa budget hearing natin, nabanggit po ng NEA Administrator, Nani Almeda,

**[00:49:50.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000532)  
na i-rescind ng NEA ang Memorandum No. 2023-52.

**[00:49:55.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000533)  
Pero alam po natin na hindi ito na-rescind.

**[00:49:58.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000534)  
And in fact, suspended lamang po ito.

**[00:50:00.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000535)  
Ang tanong ko po, bakit po hindi pa na-rescind ng NEA ang iligal na memorandum na ito?

**[00:50:05.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000536)  
Tapos kanina po kasi, I was looking at the presentation,

**[00:50:08.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000537)  
ang dami pong mga linemen na mga electric co-ops,

**[00:50:11.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000538)  
na siyang bayani natin para ma-restore ang electricity every time merong malaking sakuna sa ating bansa.

**[00:50:17.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000539)  
Pero marami po sa kanila na pagkakaitan ng collective bargaining agreement

**[00:50:21.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000540)  
dahil po dito sa inyong iligal na memorandum na ito.

**[00:50:24.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000541)  
So ma, ano po nangyari, Admin Almeda?

**[00:50:28.000] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000542)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:50:30.280] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000543)  
We have addressed the situation of the collective bargaining agreement issue.

**[00:50:38.180] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000544)  
Just for the information of the committee,

**[00:50:42.500] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000545)  
we have already sat down with the Secretary of Labor and the various groups

**[00:50:49.320] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000546)  
and we are coming up with a manualization of the Electric Cooperative Industry Tripartite Council.

**[00:50:57.520] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000547)  
Okay.

**[00:50:58.000] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000548)  
Wherein DOLE, the National Mediation Board, NEA, and the labor groups

**[00:51:04.760] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000549)  
will work together in a technical working group committee

**[00:51:10.860] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000550)  
to come up with the guidelines of the rules that will govern the collective bargaining agreement.

**[00:51:17.160] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000551)  
I beg your indulgence, sir.

**[00:51:24.700] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000552)  
We cannot...

**[00:51:26.160] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000553)  
The memorandum...

**[00:51:28.000] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000554)  
The subject memorandum is suspended.

**[00:51:30.940] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000555)  
It's suspended for the simple reason that there are peculiarities in the electric cooperative industry.

**[00:51:39.800] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000556)  
That has to be considered.

**[00:51:42.860] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000557)  
But we are trying to resolve everything through this tripartite council

**[00:51:47.520] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000558)  
as well as the due representation of the federation and the labor groups concerned.

**[00:51:54.800] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000559)  
And hopefully, we can move forward.

**[00:51:57.300] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000560)  
We have already convened two weeks ago.

**[00:52:00.440] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000561)  
Our next meeting will be on September 10.

**[00:52:02.960] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000562)  
And once we are able to come up with a resolution through the tripartite council,

**[00:52:08.500] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000563)  
everything will be within the framework of the manualization, sir.

**[00:52:14.240] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000564)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:52:15.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000565)  
Maraming salamat po, Admin Almeda.

**[00:52:17.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000566)  
Pasensya na po kayo sa impatience, no?

**[00:52:19.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000567)  
Kasi marami po ang mga nakabimbi ng mga CBA ng ating mga unions,

**[00:52:24.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000568)  
iba't iba mga power cooperatives po sa ating bansa.

**[00:52:27.300] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000569)  
Kung ito po ay sa Senado, sasabihin po sa akin nung kabilang side na asked and answered.

**[00:52:31.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000570)  
This was the same question I raised last year.

**[00:52:34.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000571)  
And more or less, this is the same answer that I'm getting this year.

**[00:52:37.760] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000572)  
Mr. Chair, just for the information, there were a lot of CBAs that were already resolved.

**[00:52:45.720] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000573)  
Medyo dun lang po talaga sa mga CBAs that will affect the very operations of the electric cooperatives.

**[00:52:54.460] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000574)  
NEA has been trying to find a way.

**[00:52:57.300] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000575)  
On how to give in to the demands in the CBA.

**[00:53:01.180] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000576)  
Mr. Chair, thank you.

**[00:53:02.660] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000577)  
Again, I'll go back to my previous question.

**[00:53:05.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000578)  
Will this illegal memorandum be rescinded?

**[00:53:10.330] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000579)

**[00:53:16.050] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000580)  
it will be rescinded, sir.

**[00:53:17.750] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-000581)  
Not only suspended.

**[00:53:19.890] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000582)  
That was the answer last year.

**[00:53:22.290] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000583)  
And it has been a year after.

**[00:53:24.170] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000584)  
Kawawa naman po yung ating mga manggagawa.

**[00:53:26.550] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000585)  
Sana po sa susunod na budget hearing,

**[00:53:28.290] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000586)  
hindi na po ito ating tanong kasi po na-rescind na ang Memorandum No. 2023-52.

**[00:53:34.350] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000587)  
We will also forward a comprehensive list of the concerns for our colleagues from Power Centro.

**[00:53:41.590] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000588)

**[00:53:44.130] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000589)

**[00:53:46.350] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000590)

**[00:53:52.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000591)  
Maraming pong salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[00:53:54.190] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000592)  
And maraming salamat po sa DOE family.

**[00:53:55.970] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000593)  
Thank you, Congressman Zendaya.

**[00:53:58.290] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000594)

**[00:54:05.250] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000595)  
Next to interpolate from the majority, Congressman Celso Rehensha.

**[00:54:11.770] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000596)  
Sir, to recognize.

**[00:54:14.150] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000597)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:54:20.490] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000598)  
I would like to raise this concern on Iligan City regarding the rising electricity costs and supply stability.

**[00:54:29.030] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000599)  
Mr. Chair, Iligan's electricity charge increased from 13 pesos and 60 centavos per kilowatt

**[00:54:37.090] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000600)  
hour in July 2026 to 15 pesos and 13 centavos per kilowatt hour in August this year also,

**[00:54:45.650] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000601)  
an increase of 1 peso and 52 centavos, or an 11.19 percent in just one month.

**[00:54:53.950] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000602)  
At this level, Iligan is among the higher rates charged by major distribution utilities in the country.

**[00:55:00.890] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000603)  
For comparison, Mr. Chair, reported rates are approximately

**[00:55:05.990] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000604)  
14.96 pesos per kilowatt hour for BICO or Visayas Electric Cooperative and 14 pesos and 78 centavos for Meralco and 12.31 pesos for Cipalco.

**[00:55:22.750] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000605)  
This is particularly concerning because Iligan, Mr. Chair, is the host of Agus 5, 6, and 7 with a combined dependable capacity of approximately 279 MW.

**[00:55:38.980] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000613)  
Mr. Chair, with this, I would like to address my concern to the PISAM through the help of the Honorable Secretary.

**[00:55:53.330] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000614)  
The following points. Number one, Mr. Chair, what was the basis for reducing the firm allocation serving Iligan to 8.75 MW, where the city's actual demand, supply needs, and the possible impact on consumers considered? Number two, can the PISAM consider providing an additional 16.25 MW of firm power, bringing the allocation serving Iligan City back to 25 MW, if feasible as an interim measure while longer-term supply alternatives are being pursued? Those are the two concerns, Mr. Chair. PISAM?

**[00:56:42.200] PSALM President and CEO Dennis Edward de la Serna** (asr-000615)  
Mr. Chair, regarding the first question on why there were reductions in our capacity allocations for certain cooperatives and distributed utilities, sir,

**[00:56:55.120] PSALM President and CEO Dennis Edward de la Serna** (asr-000616)  
we implemented a program in 2026, re-evaluation.

**[00:56:58.640] PSALM President and CEO Dennis Edward de la Serna** (asr-000617)  
We had to reduce the supply requirements because what we provide to our co-ops is based on the actual capacity of the power plants.

**[00:57:20.920] PSALM President and CEO Dennis Edward de la Serna** (asr-000618)  
And due to the reductions in the dependable capacity of Agus and Pulangi, we also had to correspondingly reduce.

**[00:57:28.720] PSALM President and CEO Dennis Edward de la Serna** (asr-000620)  
Our capacity allocations for co-ops to use, including illegal light and power.

**[00:57:34.840] PSALM President and CEO Dennis Edward de la Serna** (asr-000621)  
As to the second question, sir, if it's possible to increase the capacity allocation to illegal light and power, sir,

**[00:57:43.180] PSALM President and CEO Dennis Edward de la Serna** (asr-000622)  
as I committed to you earlier, sir, on our side meeting, sir, PISAM and our trading team will evaluate the request of illegal light

**[00:57:52.880] PSALM President and CEO Dennis Edward de la Serna** (asr-000623)  
and we will try to increase the firm and if not non-firm allocation to illegal light and power, sir.

**[00:57:58.640] PSALM President and CEO Dennis Edward de la Serna** (asr-000624)  
Thank you. Thank you very much, sir. And, Mr. Chair.

**[00:58:01.880] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000625)  
Mr. Chair, we understand, Mr. Chair, that the PISAM has limited capacity and must follow its policies.

**[00:58:10.280] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000626)  
We are not asking preferential treatment or special treatment.

**[00:58:15.460] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000627)  
We are asking for assistance for our consumers, given illegal role as host to major national power assets.

**[00:58:23.620] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000628)  
We respectfully seek a fair and practical solution that can provide greater supply,

**[00:58:28.640] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000629)  
stability, and more reasonable electricity costs.

**[00:58:32.420] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000630)  
Mr. Chair, I also raise this because the issue may not be unique to Iligan.

**[00:58:39.060] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000631)  
How firm power is allocated with the local demand and consumer impact are considered.

**[00:58:45.460] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000632)  
Could affect other districts as well.

**[00:58:47.180] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000633)  
We hope that Iligan's case can also help guide a fair approach for similarly situated communities.

**[00:58:55.620] Rep. Celso G. Regencia** (asr-000634)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. That's all.

**[00:58:58.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000635)  
Thank you. Thank you, Kong.

kayo sa

salitang

**[01:02:18.080] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000677)  
bill shock,

**[01:02:19.000] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000678)

**[01:02:36.160] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000681)

**[01:02:58.500] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000682)  
Doon po sa pagtatanggal ng VAT on system loss, ito po ay tinatrabaho na ngayon ng ERC kasama ang BIR.

**[01:03:11.760] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000683)  
Nagkaroon na nga po kami ng resolution, yung Resolution 26, Series of 2026, na kung saan aming kinlasipika ang system loss charge as a charge that is government mandated.

**[01:03:27.700] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000684)

**[01:03:34.350] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000685)  
kapareha na government mandated tulad ng

**[01:03:37.470] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000686)

**[01:03:43.220] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000687)

**[01:03:47.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000688)

**[01:03:56.960] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000689)  
at sila ngayon po ay bumabalangkas sa kanilang RMC nang sa ganon

**[01:04:02.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000690)  
ay maisakatuparan yung effectively ng pagtatanggal ng VAT on system loss

**[01:04:08.700] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000691)  
dahil yung system loss cost na ito ay hindi na mapasasama doon sa base na mapapatawan ng value-added tax.

**[01:04:18.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000692)

**[01:11:50.290] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000893)

Mr. Chair.

**[01:12:41.970] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000921)

**[01:12:45.010] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000922)

**[01:12:53.890] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000927)
