**[01:13:12.190] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000936)  
Thank you, Chair.

**[01:13:13.190] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000937)  
Thank you, Secretary Sharon.

**[01:13:16.390] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000938)  
Ilan na lang po yung tanong ko.

**[01:13:17.990] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000939)  
Ito naman tungkol sa WESM.

**[01:13:20.790] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000940)  
Meron din recently na naiulat

**[01:13:22.910] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000941)  
na mismo galing sa ERC

**[01:13:24.710] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000942)  
that there was a technical flaw

**[01:13:27.790] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000943)  
sa wholesale electricity spot market

**[01:13:30.470] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000944)  
pricing mechanism.

**[01:13:31.690] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000945)  
Ang naging resulta po,

**[01:13:33.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000946)  
ay sumobra ang nasingil

**[01:13:35.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000947)  
sa mga consumers natin,

**[01:13:37.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000948)  
especially sa Visayas area.

**[01:13:39.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000949)  
In fact, according to the reports,

**[01:13:42.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000950)  
ang masakit pa nga dito ay

**[01:13:44.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000951)  
hindi nga yung refund ay hindi binalik

**[01:13:46.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000952)  
sa mga consumers

**[01:13:48.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000953)  
kung hindi binigay sa mga power companies.

**[01:13:50.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000954)  
My first question is,

**[01:13:52.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000955)  
sa ERC,

**[01:13:54.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000956)  
magkano po ang eksaktong kabuhang

**[01:13:56.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000957)  
overcharge sa mga consumer,

**[01:13:58.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000958)  
lalo na sa Visayas grid,

**[01:14:00.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000959)  
mula nung June 2021,

**[01:14:02.850] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000960)  
dahil sa maling pricing model na ito ng WESM?

**[01:14:05.850] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000961)  
Chair Francis?

**[01:14:07.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000962)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairperson.

**[01:14:09.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000963)  
Ang nat-discovery po natin sa WESM,

**[01:14:13.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000964)  
wala pong overcharging.

**[01:14:15.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000965)  
Ibig sabihin po, tama yung mga inilalabas na

**[01:14:19.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000966)  
locational marginal prices

**[01:14:21.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000967)  
ng ating market management system.

**[01:14:24.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000968)  
Within that market management system

**[01:14:26.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000969)  
ay yung tinatawag natin market optimization

**[01:14:31.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000970)  
model na kung saan dinidetermina niya

**[01:14:35.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000971)  
ang presyo ng bawat node sa ating kabuhang grid.

**[01:14:40.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000972)  
At ito ang mga presyong gagabay ngayon

**[01:14:44.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000973)  
doon sa mga transaksyong nangyayari sa WESM.

**[01:14:47.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000974)  
Tama po ang inilalabas na presyo.

**[01:14:50.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000975)  
Pero po, ang nakita natin,

**[01:14:53.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000976)  
sa tuwing nagkakaroon ng binding constraint,

**[01:14:57.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000977)  
ibig sabihin, may congestion

**[01:15:00.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000995)  
doon sa interconnection na kumukonekta ng Visayas at Mindanao o kaya naman ng Luzon at Visayas,

**[01:15:08.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000996)  
nagkakaroon ng biglang pagsipa doon sa line rental.

**[01:15:13.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000997)  
At supposedly, kapagka nagkaroon naman ng pangungulekta ng line rental,

**[01:15:20.120] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000998)  
ito ay mapapasama doon sa bayarin ng mga distribution utilities na bumili mula sa WESEM.

**[01:15:28.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000999)  
Pero kung yung amount na malilikom mula sa lahat ng mga customers o yung tinatawag nating trading amount ng mga customers

**[01:15:38.040] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001000)  
ay sosobra doon sa pangangailangan upang mabayaran.

**[01:15:44.520] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001001)  
Yung trading amount naman, kabuoang trading amount ng para sa generators, magkakaroon ng surplus.

**[01:15:51.760] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001002)  
At nandoon sa mekanismo ng WESEM, kapagka may surplus, dapat ito ay ibabalik.

**[01:15:58.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001003)  
Doon sa mayroong kontribusyon dyan sa surplus na yan.

**[01:16:03.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001004)  
Bakit nagkakaroon ng surplus?

**[01:16:05.180] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001005)  
Mari kong ipaliwanag lang po. Hopefully, maging mas simple.

**[01:16:12.160] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001006)  
Ang ating pong locational marginal prices sa bawat node, ito po ay binubuo ng system marginal price o ito yung palang base price.

**[01:16:22.320] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001007)  
At on top of the base price, ay mayroong cost.

**[01:16:28.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001008)  
Mayroong cost of line losses at saka yung cost of congestion.

**[01:16:33.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001009)  
Bakit magkakaroon ng line losses?

**[01:16:35.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001010)  
Kasi mayroon ng inherent din na losses even sa ating transmission lines.

**[01:16:40.960] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001011)  
Kaya kung halimbawa ang isang customer, mangangailangan ng 100 megawatt, pero mayroong 2% na nawawala sa linya,

**[01:16:51.120] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001012)  
ang isang generator na magsusupply dito sa customer na ito, kakailanganin kung halimbawa 2%,

**[01:16:58.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001013)  
kakailanganin na magsusupply hindi lang ng 100 megawatt, kailangan may sobra na 102 kung 2%.

**[01:17:07.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001014)  
Dahil kung kailangan niya 100 at 100 lang ang isupply ng generator, ang mangyayari niyan, kulang.

**[01:17:16.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001015)  
Kaya mayroong kailangan siyang 2 megawatt na additional na isusupply at yung 2 megawatt na yan, may kabayaran niyan.

**[01:17:24.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001016)  
Kaya ito ay nadadagdag doon sa locational marginal price.

**[01:17:28.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001017)  
Ng node na ito, ito po ay yung isa pa lang na component, yung line loss.

**[01:17:35.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001018)  
Pagdating naman sa congestion, kung ang isang node ay masusupplyan sana ng generator na nandito sa kabilang node,

**[01:17:43.680] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001019)  
pero ang linya ay congested na, umabot na doon sa line limit na yan,

**[01:17:49.560] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001020)  
ibig sabihin kahit pa ito sana ang pinakamurang generator, para masupplyan yung pangangailangan ng customer na ito,

**[01:17:58.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001021)  
kailangan ng mayroong isang generator pa na nakakonekta sa kabilang linya na mag-generate kung anong megawatt yung hindi maitawid dito sa linya na ito.

**[01:18:11.900] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001022)  
Ito naman po ang tinatawag na congestion cost.

**[01:18:14.940] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001023)  
Ngayon po, kada node, iba-iba ang nagiging LMP.

**[01:18:20.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001024)  
Nagkakaroon ng malaking pagkakaiba.

**[01:18:24.120] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001025)  
Normally, maliit lang naman ang variance ng ating mga...

**[01:18:28.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001026)  
LMPs.

**[01:18:30.320] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001027)  
Pero ang napansin po namin, sa tuwing nagkakaroon ng binding constraint,

**[01:18:36.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001028)  
kasi po, hindi naman lahat ng generation na kailangan ng Visayas ay nage-generate sa Visayas.

**[01:18:44.260] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001029)  
Mayroon po siyang kinukuha mula sa Mindanao at ito ay dumadaloy doon sa interconnection ng Visayas at Mindanao.

**[01:18:54.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001030)  
Ngayon po, may limitasyon ang pwedeng tumawid ng megawatts doon.

**[01:18:58.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001031)  
Kaya sa tuwing ang pangangailangan ng Visayas ay napakalaki at hindi na ito kayang maitawid doon sa 450 na yan,

**[01:19:08.740] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001032)  
ang yung ating market management system, yung MDOM na yan,

**[01:19:12.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001033)  
ang ginawa niya, naglalabas siya ng magkaibang SMP or system marginal price.

**[01:19:19.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001034)  
Iba ang system marginal price doon sa Mindanao, iba ang nagiging system marginal price sa Visayas.

**[01:19:26.280] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001035)  
Mas mababa ang sa Mindanao.

**[01:19:28.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001036)  
Kasi mas mababa yung marginal plant doon.

**[01:19:31.680] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001037)  
Dito sa Visayas, mas mataas kasi nga, mas mahal yung planta, yung marginal plant na kakailanganin para masupply yung demand sa Visayas.

**[01:19:42.320] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001038)  
Dahil nagkakaiba yung system marginal price, nagiging malaki ang difference ng kanilang LMP.

**[01:19:51.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001039)  
Remember, ang SMP kung isa lang yan across all the nodes, maliit lang ang variance dahil manggagaling lang yan.

**[01:19:58.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001040)  
At saka sa congestion cost.

**[01:20:01.020] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001041)  
Ngayong nagkaroon ng SMP na iba sa Mindanao, mas mababa at mas mataas doon sa SMP ng Visayas,

**[01:20:09.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001042)  
yung mga distribution utilities natin, sila yung mga customers, halimbawa sila yung nakakonekta sa node sa Visayas.

**[01:20:17.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001043)  
Kung meron silang kontrata sa isang generation company na nasa Mindanao,

**[01:20:23.680] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001044)  
karaniwan sana mababa yung makukuha nilang kontrata.

**[01:20:28.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001045)  
Kung meron silang kuryente, batay na rin doon sa kanilang kontrata with that generation company.

**[01:20:34.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001046)  
Kaya lang, since hindi makapagpatawid ng kuryente yung galing Mindanao,

**[01:20:41.140] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001047)  
yung pangangailangan ng distribution utility na yan sa Visayas ay manggagaling sa isang planta na mas mahal na manggagaling sa Visayas.

**[01:20:50.740] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001048)  
Kaya, ang exposure niya nangyayari, meron siyang exposure sa WSM dahil in effect,

**[01:20:58.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001049)  
meron siyang kuryente na yan sa Visayas at mas mahal ang LMP sa Visayas.

**[01:21:04.040] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001050)  
Ngayon po, paano nagkakaroon ng spike sa line rental?

**[01:21:09.560] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001051)  
Meron po kasing formularyo ang line rental at ito po ay nakadepende doon sa difference ng LMP ng seller o ng generator,

**[01:21:20.840] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001052)  
yung nasa Mindanao, kasi kung ang generator ay nasa Mindanao, at yung LMP ng customer sa Visayas.

**[01:21:28.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001053)  
Ang ito po ay malaki, malaki rin po ang nakakalculate na line rental.

**[01:21:34.980] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001054)  
Pero, wala pong mali doon, wala pong overcharge doon.

**[01:21:39.720] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001055)  
Ang nangyayari lang po sa ating price determination methodology na umiiral sa WSM,

**[01:21:46.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001056)  
ang nakokolektang amount mula doon sa customer na nasa Visayas, dahil nakabasingado sa LMP niya sa Visayas,

**[01:21:56.720] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001057)  
mas malaki yung trading amount.

**[01:21:58.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001058)  
Ang nakokolekta from the customers dito sa Visayas, mas malaki kaysa doon sa dapat sanang ibayad sa mga generator.

**[01:22:06.380] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001059)  
Dahil ang kanila namang nodal price, yung LMP nila ay nasa Mindanao.

**[01:22:10.940] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001060)  
Ang pagbalansi sana yan, para mabawi nung na-expose sa mataas na line rental,

**[01:22:19.320] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001061)

**[02:00:00.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001958)  
under Section 43 of EPIRA was made possible through the issuance of the RDWR and RTWR.

**[02:00:09.880] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001959)  
Ano po itong performance-based and how does this consider yung recovery ng just and reasonable cost ng ating mga distribution utilities?

**[02:00:20.320] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001960)  
Ito pong performance-based regulation ay isang forward-looking na methodology unlike RORB na nakabase sa historical cost or historical test here.

**[02:00:32.980] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001961)  
Dahil po ito ay forward-looking, ito naman po may mga built-in na mga incentive mechanisms sa ganitong metodolohiya.

**[02:00:41.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001962)  
Bakit po? Ang pag-determine na po ng revenue requirement under performance-based regulation,

**[02:00:47.960] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001963)  
kapareho rin naman po ang component niyan.

**[02:00:49.880] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001964)  
Tulad nung sa revenue requirement ng RORB.

**[02:00:53.320] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001965)  
Titingnan po yung kanilang OPEX.

**[02:00:55.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001966)  
Ito po yung mga building blocks.

**[02:00:56.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001967)  
OPEX, return on rate base, return off rate base, at saka yung other taxes.

**[02:01:07.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001968)  
Yung pong pagtatakda ng revenue requirement using forecast.

**[02:01:12.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001969)  
Ito po yung forecast na base doon sa efficient operating expenses,

**[02:01:17.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001970)  
at saka yung rate base niya, yung nakalipas na ng mga na-procure niya na assets,

**[02:01:26.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001971)  
and at the same time, yung mga ipoprocure pa niya through CAPEX during the regulatory period of either 4 years or 5 years in the case of NGCP.

**[02:01:38.140] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001972)  
For distribution utilities, ang nangyayari po during a rate reset exercise,

**[02:01:43.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001973)  
ay tinitingnan po yung nararapat na OPEX.

**[02:01:47.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001974)  
At saka kung ano yung magiging value na gagamitin para sa regulatory asset base, yung regulated entity.

**[02:01:55.940] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001975)  
Ito pong regulatory asset base will consist of yung roll forward na assets.

**[02:02:01.560] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001976)  
Ito po yung nanggaling na yung dati pang mga assets na i-roll forward.

**[02:02:06.200] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001977)  
Kasama na rin po dito yung mga CAPEX and other assets na na-procure prior to the rate reset.

**[02:02:15.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001978)  
Pagkatapos po noon,

**[02:02:17.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001979)  
ay de-determine na kung ano yung magiging value ng return on rate base at saka return off rate base.

**[02:02:25.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001980)  
Mr. Chair,

**[02:02:26.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001981)  
bakit po mag...

**[02:02:26.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001982)  
Ilan pong countries ang gumagamit pa nung ganyang sistema?

**[02:02:36.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001983)  
Sa bilang po, wala po akong isaktong datos.

**[02:02:39.780] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001984)  
Pero po, ang sapagkakalam ko, nung ito po ay inaaral,

**[02:02:43.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001985)  
ang mga ibang jurisdiction na gumagamit nito will include...

**[02:02:47.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001986)  
UK, Australia, New Zealand.

**[02:02:53.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001987)  
So dito nga po talaga nakapattern yung ating PBR.

**[02:02:57.220] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001988)  
Kailan po ba nagbago yung from RORB?

**[02:03:02.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001989)  
Kasi ang kaya ko po tinatanong, hindi po ba dapat yung utility, lalo na pag private,

**[02:03:08.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001990)  
dapat sila ang nagpo-put up ng capital at saka yung investment bago nila i-recover sa mga consumers?

**[02:03:16.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001991)  
Opo, tama po yan.

**[02:03:17.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001992)  
Kaya nga din po, sa ating PBR ngayon,

**[02:03:21.880] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001993)  
ang kanila pong CAPEX,

**[02:03:24.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001994)  
pag na ito ay napasama doon sa kanilang rate reset na aprobahan,

**[02:03:30.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001995)  
yung pong kanilang return,

**[02:03:32.700] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001996)  
yung kanilang recovery,

**[02:03:34.220] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001997)  
hindi po nakabatay doon sa kabuang halaga nung CAPEX na yan.

**[02:03:38.780] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001998)  
Ito pong kanilang ika-CAPEX na,

**[02:03:41.720] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-001999)  
magpo-procure sila ng linya o ng transformer,

**[02:03:47.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002000)  
yung pong halaga na yun, kung papasok yan doon sa regulatory period na yan in the form of CAPEX,

**[02:03:52.900] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002001)  
maitatayo yan, ang kikitain lang po noon,

**[02:03:55.760] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002002)  
hindi po yung makukolekta po as part of the revenue requirement,

**[02:04:00.140] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002003)  
is not the entire value of that CAPEX,

**[02:04:02.560] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002004)  
but it will be the value of that asset at yung return yan will depend on the WAC that will be approved

**[02:04:10.060] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002005)  
o yung weighted average cost of capital para doon sa return on rate base

**[02:04:15.300] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002006)  
at saka yung kaukulang.

**[02:04:16.920] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002007)  
At yung depreciation na yan, recoverable yan over the life of the asset.

**[02:04:22.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002008)  
Paano kung mangyayari if the project is delayed or hindi nagawa?

**[02:04:28.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002009)  
Meron na po bang sitwasyon na nakasingil based dito sa current na sistema,

**[02:04:34.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002010)  
tapos na-delay o hindi nagawa yung project?

**[02:04:37.740] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002011)  
Dito po pumapasok yung kahalagahan ng rate reset exercise.

**[02:04:43.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002012)  
After every regulatory period of 4 years or 5 years,

**[02:04:46.920] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002013)  
pinakailangan on the final year, magkakaroon ng rate reset.

**[02:04:52.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002014)  
Ano po yung purpose ng rate reset na yan?

**[02:04:54.500] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002015)  
Para magkwentahan, magkakaroon ng kwentas-klaras,

**[02:04:58.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002016)  
ikaw ba utility, nagawa mo ba yung CAPEX mo?

**[02:05:01.220] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002017)  
E meron lang doon sa iyong taripa, nakasama doon yung return on at saka return off.

**[02:05:06.620] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002018)  
Kung hindi mo nagawa yan, dito po sa susunod na rate reset,

**[02:05:10.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002019)  
yan po ngayon ipo-flowback.

**[02:05:12.140] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002020)  
Mangyayari po, meron kang CAPEX na delay ka,

**[02:05:16.920] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002021)  
o hindi mo nagawa, dito sa susunod na rate reset,

**[02:05:20.280] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002022)  
ito ay ibabawa sa inyong revenue requirement.

**[02:05:22.840] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002023)  
Meron ka pang penalty, meron ka pang interest na dapat bayaran doon.

**[02:05:26.800] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002024)  
Ito lang po ang hindi nangyari dahil sa loob ng mahigit isang dekada,

**[02:05:31.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002025)  
ang ERC noon, hindi nakapag-reset ng taripa ng ating mga private distribution utilities.

**[02:05:37.260] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002026)  
At ngayong taon lang po natin ito sinasagawa,

**[02:05:40.620] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002027)  
at nagkaroon na nga po ang pagdedesisyon ng ERC,

**[02:05:43.380] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002028)  
sa loob ng mahigit isang dekada,

**[02:05:46.920] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002029)  
una-unahang rate reset sa isang ating private distribution utilities.

**[02:05:51.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002030)  
So, Mr. Chair, marami po tayong nakita na mga ganong sitwasyon

**[02:05:55.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002031)  
na nasinil na po sa consumers yung CAPEX para doon sa proyekto.

**[02:06:00.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002032)  
Pero hindi po tayo nakapag-rate reset. Tama po ba?

**[02:06:05.130] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002033)  
Ang nangyari po, dahil nga po,

**[02:06:07.730] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002034)  
ngayong taon lang talaga nagsimula ulit ang rate reset ng ating mga private distribution utilities,

**[02:06:13.630] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002035)  
ngayon lang din po natin tinitingnan,

**[02:06:16.330] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002036)  
dahil in between,

**[02:06:18.390] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002037)  
nagkaroon din po sila ng mga CAPEX filings.

**[02:06:21.150] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002038)  
Kasi supposedly, sa rate reset magkakaroon ng approval ng CAPEX program

**[02:06:25.750] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002039)  
ng ating regulated entity.

**[02:06:28.210] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002040)  
Dahil nga po naantala yung rate reset,

**[02:06:30.950] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002041)  
itong mga distribution utilities na kakailangan ng mag-implement ng CAPEX,

**[02:06:35.230] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002042)  
nagsipag-file sila ng kanilang mga individual CAPEX filings.

**[02:06:40.390] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002043)  
At kung na-approbahan at isinagawa nila yung CAPEX project na yan,

**[02:06:43.870] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002044)  
sila'y gumastos na, pero hindi sila nagkaroon ng rate adjustment.

**[02:06:48.390] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002045)  
Dahil nga, walang rate reset na nangyari.

**[02:06:50.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002046)  
So ngayong pong taon na ito,

**[02:06:52.450] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002047)  
dito sa ating rate reset exercise na ginagawa para sa ating mga private distribution utilities,

**[02:06:58.110] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002048)  
ating itinatama yung lahat ng nangyaring walang aksyon,

**[02:07:03.250] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002049)  
kawalang aksyon,

**[02:07:04.310] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002050)  
noong mahigit isang dekada,

**[02:07:06.630] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002051)  
ngayon po ay itinatama natin.

**[02:07:08.270] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002052)  
Nagsaganon, ay makasiguro tayo na yung magiging singil ng ating mga distribution utilities

**[02:07:13.690] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002053)  
ay nararapat lang sa kanilang kaukalong gastos

**[02:07:17.050] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002054)  
at kung alo lamang,

**[02:07:18.390] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002055)  
yung reasonable.

**[02:07:19.390] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002056)  
Mr. Chair,

**[02:07:20.210] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002057)  
Thank you.

**[02:07:20.710] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002058)  
Sa rate reset po ba?

**[02:07:21.710] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002059)  
Last answer for the last question.

**[02:07:23.710] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002060)  
Tumakbo yata yung oras ko kanina, Mr. Chair.

**[02:07:26.710] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002061)  
I'll give you another one.

**[02:07:27.710] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002062)  
Go ahead.

**[02:07:28.210] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002063)  
Just a short question.

**[02:07:30.210] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002064)  
Sa rate reset po ba, Mr. Chair,

**[02:07:33.210] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002065)  
kung nasingil po natin sa consumers yan,

**[02:07:36.210] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002066)  
doon sa rate reset,

**[02:07:38.210] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002067)  
ibabalik po ba with interest yan?

**[02:07:40.210] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002068)  
Ang mangyayari po,

**[02:07:43.490] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002069)  
dahil po ito ay ipo-flow back,

**[02:07:45.490] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002070)  
doon po sa revenue requirement na itatakda para sa susunod na

**[02:07:49.510] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002071)  
regulatory period,

**[02:07:51.510] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002072)  
ibabawas na yan.

**[02:07:53.510] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002073)  
So, in effect, ibinabalik na din yan

**[02:07:55.510] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002074)  
doon sa susunod na regulatory period po.

**[02:07:57.510] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002075)  
So, ibabalik din yan sa consumers with interest.

**[02:08:00.510] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002076)  
Doon sa ating...

**[02:08:02.510] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002077)  
Just a short manifestation.

**[02:08:04.510] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002078)  
So, Mr. Chair,

**[02:08:06.510] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002079)  
for me, investments are a business risk.

**[02:08:08.510] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002080)  
Consumers should pay a fair and reasonable

**[02:08:10.510] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-002081)  
return for investments

**[02:16:28.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002288)  
I'll give you the answer.

**[02:16:30.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002289)  
Sir, sir. Caramoan. Distrito ko po yun.

**[02:16:33.540] National Transmission Corporation President and CEO Atty. Joseph Omar A. Castillo** (asr-002290)  
In the TDP, I believe it's presentation.

**[02:16:35.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002291)  
Yeah. From Karamuan, malapit na lang po kami doon. Actually, meron pong feasibility study na nakuha na kami na gagawa po ng tulay from Karamuan connecting Katanduanes.

**[02:16:48.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002292)  
And kinausap ko na po yung DPWH, yung ilalim nun. Pwede po tayo maglagay doon ng linya na. So, para hindi na submarine cable, mas mahal po yun.

**[02:16:58.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002293)  
So, yung design na pinapagawa po namin is for the maintenance. It's a long-term plan, no? So, it's one option. I'm just telling you na yung pinakamalapit.

**[02:17:10.040] National Transmission Corporation President and CEO Atty. Joseph Omar A. Castillo** (asr-002294)  
We'll take a look at that, Mr. Chair.

**[02:17:11.440] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002295)  
Mr. Chair, in addition to the schedule of the TDP, Transmission Development Plan of the NGCP, DOE also has issued a circular allowing third-party,

**[02:17:27.600] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002296)  
allowing third-party,

**[02:17:28.120] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002297)  
construction. But we have to work closely with NGCP on this. Transcos already identified three projects that they will embark on with the permission or the agreement to NGCP also with DOE.

**[02:17:44.100] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002298)  
We'll try as much as possible makuha yung approval for, para ma-advance po, na transco na yung magpapatayo. We'll work hard on that, sir.

**[02:17:54.860] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002299)  
Dalawang beses na ako bumalik din dahil sa problema dyan.

**[02:17:58.120] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002300)  
Maybe we can find a long-term solution, sir.

**[02:18:00.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002301)  
Thank you.

**[02:18:01.040] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002302)

**[02:18:10.040] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002303)  
Yun talagang problema namin. I hope itong aming tulungan ni Kung Fuente Villa na yung sa aming Princip Bridge na talagang pangarap din naman namin yun magkaroon na may tawid.

**[02:18:23.040] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002304)

**[02:18:25.700] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002305)

**[02:18:28.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002306)

**[02:18:30.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002307)  
Basta pag nagsalita si Kong Sharon, Kong Lagrales, maniwala po tayo.

**[02:18:37.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002308)

**[02:18:44.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002309)

**[02:19:00.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002312)

**[02:19:09.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002313)

**[02:19:18.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002314)  
So, maganda yan kasi we recognize them.

**[02:19:21.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002315)

**[02:19:25.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002316)

**[02:19:29.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002317)

**[02:19:32.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002318)

**[02:19:38.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002319)

**[02:19:44.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002320)

**[02:19:50.740] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002321)  
Mr. Chair? Mr. Chair?

**[02:19:52.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002322)

**[02:19:54.060] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002323)  
I just wanted to intervene, Mr. Chair.

**[02:19:56.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002324)  
Yes, yes.

**[02:19:56.940] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002325)  
For us, in the Department of Energy

**[02:19:59.100] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002326)  
And I think especially for NEA, we don't need a special day for linemen.

**[02:20:05.800] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002327)  
Every day is a day for linemen, sir.

**[02:20:08.260] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002328)  
Because not only do they build our infrastructure, but they are the first to go whenever there's a disaster.

**[02:20:16.480] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002329)  
Mga volunteers yan.

**[02:20:17.600] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002330)  
They don't have anywhere to sleep.

**[02:20:19.300] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002331)  
They just follow where the broken poles are.

**[02:20:22.520] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002332)  
Mga volunteer po yan.

**[02:20:23.620] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002333)  
Pinapakain lang ng mayor.

**[02:20:24.820] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002334)  
But I think they really need commendation in that sense.

**[02:20:28.740] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002335)  
Because they are really the heroes of the energy family, sir.

**[02:20:33.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002336)  
Well noted.

**[02:20:34.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002337)  
Thank you, Chairman.

**[02:20:35.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002338)  
And of course, to Secretary.

**[02:20:36.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002339)  
Again, to Secretary, when Kong Percy said a while ago, he asked you personally kung paano baaba ang kuryente.

**[02:20:45.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002340)  
And you said, there's a formula.

**[02:20:48.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002341)  
Siguro, nung 18 Congress kami, yan na inaan namin talaga hindi bumaba ba.

**[02:20:52.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002342)  
Aside from the inflation rate.

**[02:20:54.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002343)  
So, siguro this time, yun nga, yung system loss natin.

**[02:20:58.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002344)  
Tanong ko, is it time ng generation, the gencos, should be regulated na?

**[02:21:06.810] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002345)  
Although we know in EPIRA, hindi.

**[02:21:09.630] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002346)  
Pero, it's high time, is it?

**[02:21:12.090] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002347)  
Mr. Chair, I do not think that we need to regulate.

**[02:21:17.850] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002348)  
But we need to have safeguards, Mr. Chair.

**[02:21:21.390] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002349)  
For example, once you have a COC, you have the permit, you have the permit forever.

**[02:21:29.230] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002350)  
Regardless of your performance.

**[02:21:31.410] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002351)  
And that's why we are issuing new policies now to have more accountability for the gencos.

**[02:21:41.070] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002352)  
Kasi minsan, nakalista yan sa profile natin, sa energy mix natin.

**[02:21:46.830] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002353)  
Pero, mga four months a year lang pala siya nandun.

**[02:21:50.310] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002354)  
And there has to be accountability.

**[02:21:52.030] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002355)  
Dapat siya nagahanap ng replacement power.

**[02:21:53.990] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002356)  
So, there are things that have been missing over the years.

**[02:21:58.610] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002357)  
And we...

**[02:21:59.230] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002358)  
We need to be more strict in a sense that they have to comply with whatever is required by EPIRA,

**[02:22:06.090] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002359)  
whatever is required by ERC, and the PSA or the contract they entered.

**[02:22:10.250] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002360)  
So, papunta na kami dyan, Mr. Chair.

**[02:22:13.350] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002361)  
And I think if we do regulate, we might dislodge the investment appetite in that sense.

**[02:22:26.710] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002362)  
Pero, kailangan talaga medyo may mga regulator.

**[02:22:29.230] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002363)  
Sorry, powers tayo.

**[02:22:30.910] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002364)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:22:32.750] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002365)  
Of course, when Congressman Jokno asked about this solar para sa bayan,

**[02:22:39.570] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002366)  
noong 18th Congress, di pa kami congressman ni Kogdagi.

**[02:22:42.730] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002367)  
Kami ang nagra-rally na huwag pagbigyan yan.

**[02:22:45.690] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002368)  
Kasi alam namin mangyayari.

**[02:22:46.910] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002369)  
Look what happened to Occidental Mindoro.

**[02:22:49.650] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002370)  
Kawain lang nilagay nilang poste.

**[02:22:51.550] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002371)  
So, when you said, you know, balitan-balitan, 24 billion nilang ano.

**[02:22:58.070] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002372)  
I think hindi yun ang problem.

**[02:22:59.230] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002373)  
Ang problema dun, did they deliver what is stated in their service contract na magdi-deliver siya ng supply?

**[02:23:08.260] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002374)  
You're correct, Mr. Chair, in a sense that because of, how many megawatts yun?

**[02:23:15.700] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002375)  
These are about 33 contracts.

**[02:23:18.940] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002376)

**[02:23:21.300] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002377)  
And had those, it's about 1.3 gigawatts.

**[02:23:26.460] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002378)  
Had those 1.3 gigawatts been delivered,

**[02:23:29.340] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002379)  
according to our simulation, there would have been a decrease in the price, about 2 pesos ba?

**[02:23:36.520] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002380)  
1 peso.

**[02:23:37.700] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002381)  
1 peso at the very least on the generation rate natin, sir.

**[02:23:42.220] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002382)  
So, that's, it's not the penalty itself, but it's the cost to our consumers na nangyari.

**[02:23:49.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002383)  
See, that's the problem, Chairman, because you gave the service contract.

**[02:23:52.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002384)  
So, walang pwedeng umago dun until such time na mag-deliver sila ng ano.

**[02:23:56.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002385)  
So, do you see?

**[02:23:59.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002386)  
In the foreseeable future, magkakaroon kayo ng cancellation of service contracts na hindi pa na-umpisan?

**[02:24:05.400] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002387)  
Mr. Chair, all the contracts we are being reviewed, we're cleaning it up.

**[02:24:11.480] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002388)  
We have cancelled so far about 100 plus, 200 contracts already.

**[02:24:17.240] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002389)  
That's for the service contracts on renewable energy.

**[02:24:20.020] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002390)  
Yung hindi gumagalaw, yung wall, late na siya, or wala siyang, ano tawag dito?

**[02:24:28.020] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002391)  
Hindi siya may-aral.

**[02:24:29.100] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002392)  
Hindi siya may-aral ng lote.

**[02:24:29.800] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002393)  
Wala siyang tenorial, ano?

**[02:24:31.680] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002394)  
Posesory, right.

**[02:24:33.180] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002395)  
So, we're cancelling, starting to cancel that.

**[02:24:35.960] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002396)  
And then, on the, and then next is we issue the GENCO accountability na kailangan may warning naman.

**[02:24:43.940] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002397)  
Pag hindi ka mag-comply ng requirements ng DOE or ERC, strike 1 ka.

**[02:24:48.540] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002398)  
Next strike, up to strike 3 ka, we can cancel the COE or blacklist you.

**[02:24:56.560] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002399)  
So, we are trying to have more discipline.

**[02:24:59.100] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-002400)  
In the generation companies, Mr. Chair.

**[02:25:03.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002401)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:25:04.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002402)

**[02:25:06.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002403)

**[02:25:09.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002404)

**[02:25:15.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002405)

**[02:25:19.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002406)

**[02:25:27.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002407)  
Yan siya.

**[02:25:32.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002408)

**[02:25:33.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-002409)  
If our director for...

Ang mga naka-outage po sa Visayas lalo na would include the Therma Visayas at yung PEDC.

**[02:26:02.560] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002414)

**[02:26:10.130] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002415)

**[02:26:14.730] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002416)  
Alam mo kung ilang planta and how many megawatts na hindi sila nakapag-deliver?

**[02:26:22.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002417)  
A big thing.

**[02:26:27.320] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002418)

**[02:26:28.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002419)  
Yung each unit of TVI is 176.

**[02:26:31.940] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002420)  
So, dalawa sila.

**[02:26:32.720] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002421)  
Kaya, nung ngayong naka-outage sila, medyo malaki talaga yung nawawala sa available supplies sa Visayas lalo na.

**[02:26:41.560] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002422)  
That's why we are seeing the yellow...

**[02:26:44.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002423)  
red alerts in the region.

**[02:26:48.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002424)  
Alam mo, dito tayo naintindihan natin line rental eh.

**[02:26:51.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002425)  
Can you explain yung layman's term?

**[02:26:53.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002426)  
You suspended August 16 yung line rental, di ba?

**[02:26:59.260] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002427)  
Yes po, Mr. Chair.

**[02:27:00.780] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002428)  
During periods where the interconnection is congested, Visayas and Mindanao,

**[02:27:09.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002429)  
dahil doon natin nakikita nagkakaroon ng spike sa line rental,

**[02:27:14.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002430)  
we asked IMOP to suspend the calculation and collection of the line rental

**[02:27:18.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002431)  
and then just collect the necessary amounts from the customers

**[02:27:23.180] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002432)  
para mabayaran yung mga generators.

**[02:27:25.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002433)  
But as a long-term solution, there is something to be done doon sa data

**[02:27:32.940] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002434)  
that will be inputted in the CRSS so that the correct allocation of NSS surplus

**[02:27:44.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002435)  
to the customers will be done.

**[02:27:47.880] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002436)  
And we also ask IMOP to recalculate, to cost the audit through a third party

**[02:27:55.540] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002437)  
of the payments or the NSS allocations made over the period from 2021

**[02:28:04.040] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002438)  
so that we can determine if there will be a need to order some refunds

**[02:28:12.140] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002439)  
in favor of...

**[02:28:14.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002440)  
some distribution utilities who should have received a higher percentage

**[02:28:18.720] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-002441)  
or higher allocation of the NSS.

**[02:28:22.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002442)  
Thank you.

**[02:28:23.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002443)

**[02:28:25.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002444)

We just issued a...

**[03:10:25.960] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003729)

**[03:10:28.200] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003730)

**[03:10:38.380] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003735)

**[03:10:40.320] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003736)

**[03:10:54.700] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003743)

**[03:10:56.680] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003744)

**[03:10:58.860] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003745)

**[03:11:06.140] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-003748)

**[03:11:07.980] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-003749)

**[03:11:09.760] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-003750)

**[03:11:12.040] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-003751)  
except those that are

**[03:11:13.860] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-003752)

**[03:11:16.500] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-003753)

**[03:11:17.960] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-003754)

**[03:11:20.080] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-003755)

**[03:11:22.680] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-003756)  
So, even if we turn off

**[03:15:27.630] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003869)  
Last about, with regard to the fuel issue.

**[03:15:32.050] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003870)  
As of April 2026, DOE reported approximately 49 days of diesel

**[03:15:38.990] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003871)  
and 54 days of gasoline supply.

**[03:15:42.590] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003872)  
But those figures largely represent overall national inventories

**[03:15:46.090] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003873)  
rather than necessarily government-owned strategic stocks.

**[03:15:50.670] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003874)  
Of the countries reported 49 days of diesel inventory,

**[03:15:54.370] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003875)  
how many days are actually owned or controlled by the government?

**[03:15:57.790] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003876)  
And where will PNOC physically store the reserve?

**[03:16:02.050] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003877)  
Yes, actually, the government doesn't normally buy stock, no?

**[03:16:09.390] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003878)  
We did this in this time of emergency.

**[03:16:12.310] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003879)  
We bought, essentially, the government's hold, the one we bought,

**[03:16:16.110] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003880)  
five days of diesel stock only.

**[03:16:19.330] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003881)  
And then less than a day of stock of gasoline.

**[03:16:23.770] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003882)  
At the present time, the government doesn't have its own facility.

**[03:16:27.030] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003883)  
We rented storage.

**[03:16:30.290] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003884)  
In one of the private areas.

**[03:16:32.050] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003885)  
Private storage companies.

**[03:16:34.650] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003886)  
And, in fact, there was a discussion about strategic petroleum reserve,

**[03:16:41.490] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003887)  
which the Congress is now looking into.

**[03:16:45.530] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003888)  
There are committee hearings.

**[03:16:47.010] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003889)  
There are drafts.

**[03:16:48.110] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003890)  
Because we have seen the importance of the government role.

**[03:16:53.450] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003891)  
But we have to define what is the role of the government.

**[03:16:56.430] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003892)  
Because we still don't want to intrude too much private enterprise.

**[03:17:01.670] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003893)  
Because,

**[03:17:02.050] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003894)  
we need a lot of money for the negotiation of the fuel.

**[03:17:06.930] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003895)  
So, we have to be strategic about where to allocate the money of the government

**[03:17:13.090] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-003896)  
when we establish the strategic petroleum reserve.

**[03:17:17.370] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-003897)  
Mr. Chair.

**[03:17:18.190] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-003898)  
Yes.

**[03:17:18.950] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-003899)

**[03:17:26.570] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-003901)

**[03:17:52.470] PNOC President and CEO Franz Josef George E. Alvarez** (asr-003910)

**[03:18:02.050] PNOC President and CEO Franz Josef George E. Alvarez** (asr-003913)

**[03:18:16.370] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003918)

**[03:18:31.430] PNOC President and CEO Franz Josef George E. Alvarez** (asr-003924)

**[03:18:41.630] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-003925)

**[03:18:44.150] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-003926)

**[03:32:27.880] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004215)  
renewable energy program

**[03:32:28.760] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004216)  
particular sa solar energy acceleration na tutugon sa ating pangangailangan ng alternative energy source.

**[03:32:37.840] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004217)  
Sabi kasi ni Secretary Francisco Laurel na may proposal siya ng geopensing around 3 million hectares of flat agricultural land,

**[03:32:49.140] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004218)  
particularly rice-producing areas, to protect prime prime land as the Philippines accelerates its shift toward renewable energy

**[03:32:58.060] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004219)  
and prepares for the next Green Energy Auction or tinatawag natin GEA.

**[03:33:04.480] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004220)  
Secretary Green, how will the DOE meet its National Renewable Energy Program or tinatawag natin in RIP capacity targets for 2027?

**[03:33:19.040] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004221)  
Kung ang significant portion ng plant ground mounted solar projects ay nare-restrict due to the Department of Agriculture's geopensing,

**[03:33:28.060] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004222)  
ang criteria. Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:33:29.500] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004223)  
Yes, sir. We've had a discussion already with the DA Secretary, but Yusek Ibarra can answer the question, Mr. Chair.

**[03:33:37.600] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004224)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:33:40.200] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004225)  
Yun pong mga solar power plants natin, required po silang magpakita ng proof of possessory rights.

**[03:33:49.240] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004226)  
Ngayon po, una, bawal magtayo ng solar sa mayroong tubig, irrigation. Bawal po yan.

**[03:33:58.060] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004227)  
Rice fields po, may irrigation. So, hindi po talaga kasama yan.

**[03:34:02.760] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004228)  
Sa ngayon po, wala naman po kaming developer na nagsasabi na tatamaan sila noong agri-fencing na gagawin ng Department of Agriculture.

**[03:34:12.520] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004229)  
So, hindi po kami nagtatayo ng planta doon sa pwede pong gamitin for productive agricultural activities. Thank you, po.

**[03:34:22.120] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004230)  
Sir, I just want to add also.

**[03:34:24.620] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004231)  
Okay, sure.

**[03:34:25.960] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004232)  
Meron din. Tama naman ginagamitin.

**[03:34:28.060] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004233)  
Ito ang gawa ng DAA. Pero we also go around to the LGUs.

**[03:34:32.820] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004234)  
Ang pinopropose namin din sa kanila, they can actually issue ordinances na i-limit din nila para hindi affected yung food production nila.

**[03:34:42.540] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004235)  
They can do that also on their own. Kasi kahit mabigyan namin yan ng kontrata, dadaan pa rin naman yan sa LGU at kailangan nila ng permit from the LGU.

**[03:34:52.500] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004236)  
Thank you, Secretary, for that response.

**[03:34:55.240] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004237)  
Sa ilalim po kasi ng pinagsamang Farm to Energy Framework, anong bahagi ng allocation, yung allotment nyo ng DOE para sa 2027, ang direktang sumusuporta sa pag-uugnay ng mga suppliers ng renewable energy sa ilalim ng Green Energy Option Program or DOP upang mapababa ang halaga ng karyente para sa patubig, cold storage at mga pasilidad para sa post-harvest.

**[03:35:23.560] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004238)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:35:25.240] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004239)  
Mr. Chair, meron po tayong Green Energy Option, GOP. Meron din po tayong ARCOA, Retail Competition and Open Access.

**[03:35:35.540] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004240)  
Yung po sa Retail Competition and Open Access, last June 26 po, ibinaba po ng ERC to 100 kilowatts ang threshold.

**[03:35:43.640] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004241)  
Yung naman pong Green Energy Option, binaba po ng DOE to 50 kilowatts.

**[03:35:48.380] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004242)  
Ngayon po, pag nasa isang prangkisa lang lahat ng mga under sa Department of Agriculture,

**[03:35:55.240] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004243)  
nilang mag-avail noong tinatawag na Retail Aggregation Program.

**[03:36:00.120] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004244)  
Pag pinagsama-sama mo sila, aabot na po sila doon sa threshold nitong Retail Competition and Open Access sa CAGIO.

**[03:36:06.960] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004245)  
Pwede na po silang pumili kung sino po yung magiging supplier nila na renewable energy.

**[03:36:12.780] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004246)

**[03:36:19.420] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-004247)  
So, pwede po mapababa ang presyo ng kuryente nila.
