**[03:42:11.590] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004318)  
to really widen the road.

**[03:42:15.210] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004319)  
However, it was estimated like 50 million yung cost of the poles na marire-remove

**[03:42:21.330] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004320)  
and marire-relocate.

**[03:42:23.210] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004321)  
So as of the moment, I believe the Samalcoan has already spent like 13 million.

**[03:42:31.010] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004322)  
So parang tumigil na sila dahil since 2023 pa, hindi siya nababayaran.

**[03:42:37.130] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004323)  
So ano kaya ang maganda natin gawin dito po?

**[03:42:41.270] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004324)  
Mr. Chair, Kong, I believe me and the Secretary will sit down with Secretary Vince Dizon

**[03:42:49.450] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004325)  
on September next week, no?

**[03:42:54.810] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004326)  
Nakipag-usap na rin po ako sa DPWH kasi marami na rin talagang utang ang DPWH

**[03:43:01.010] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004327)  
pole relocation.

**[03:43:02.510] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004328)  
So during my last meeting with the USEC ni Conti,

**[03:43:08.030] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004329)  
we requested an allocation of at least 300 million

**[03:43:13.910] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004330)  
to cover the pole relocation advances of the electric cooperatives.

**[03:43:20.310] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004331)  
Kasi sa 2026 yata, 50 million lang na-allocate ang DPWH.

**[03:43:25.630] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004332)  
But I'll take note of this, Mr. Chair,

**[03:43:27.890] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004333)  
and on those...

**[03:43:31.010] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004334)  
lane expansions, I'll make sure that it will be first among the list.

**[03:43:37.550] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004335)  
Mr. Chair, the pole relocation funding is actually through the DPWH

**[03:43:42.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004336)  
and we have a MOA, no?

**[03:43:44.730] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004337)  
With them, na kahit na yan na yung mag-implement, basta i-refund lang yung gastos.

**[03:43:51.830] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004338)  
Thank you and good news siguro yun na at least ma-accelerate na natin.

**[03:43:55.710] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004339)  
I will be coordinating with the office of the administrator

**[03:43:59.710] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004340)  
for the...

**[03:44:01.010] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004341)  
relocation of the poles.

**[03:44:04.050] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004342)  
And,

**[03:44:04.390] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004343)  
Mr. Chair,

**[03:44:07.130] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004344)  
I would also like to raise an issue also.

**[03:44:10.350] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004345)  
There is one barangay na very near lang sa mainland,

**[03:44:16.790] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004346)  
but however,

**[03:44:18.710] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004347)  
since bagyohin yung lugar namin,

**[03:44:21.850] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004348)  
parati siya napuputol yung...

**[03:44:23.970] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004349)  
natutumba yung pole.

**[03:44:26.370] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004350)  
So if we can request that this island,

**[03:44:29.970] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004351)  
para hindi na siya nagparatay,

**[03:44:31.010] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004352)  
that constantly nagba-burnout,

**[03:44:32.530] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004353)  
kasi mga roughly 1.5 kilometers lang from the mainland,

**[03:44:37.830] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004354)  
baka pwede natin maging submersible,

**[03:44:39.850] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004355)  
is it possible po ba na may mga programa pa kayong na

**[03:44:42.910] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004356)  
ma-convert natin yung pole nila into a submersible nila

**[03:44:47.430] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004357)  
so that it will be more resilient sa mga typhoons?

**[03:44:50.950] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004358)  
Yes, sir, it's possible.

**[03:44:51.930] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004359)  
We just need to do a cost-benefit analysis.

**[03:44:55.070] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004360)  
Baka naman continuing consumption tapos malaki yung investment.

**[03:45:00.000] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004361)  
If it does not work, nalagyan natin ng submarine cables, then we can deploy a different technology in the area.

**[03:45:11.260] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004362)  
We'll have it checked, sir.

**[03:45:12.640] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004363)  
We'll talk to your team para makuha lang namin yung mga pangalan ng area, sir.

**[03:45:17.000] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004364)  
Thank you.

**[03:45:17.640] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004365)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:45:18.740] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004366)  
And there is also a solar project in the Samalcoan that the European Union has donated, like roughly 60 million.

**[03:45:34.620] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004367)  
So at least we get to enjoy na free na makatulong din siya na mababa na yung rate natin sa per kilowatt hour.

**[03:45:43.360] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004368)  
But however, is it possible that we can waive the fees?

**[03:45:48.360] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004369)  
Kasi nagbabayad po yung Samalcoan.

**[03:45:51.880] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004370)  
Ako din ng roughly 100,000 a month sa mga, although kahit libre lang na binigay yung,

**[03:46:00.940] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004371)  
dinonate lang yung, tawag nito, solar plant.

**[03:46:07.060] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004372)  
But however, medyo nagsisingil din yung DOA.

**[03:46:10.720] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004373)  
Is it possible po ba na we can waive?

**[03:46:14.660] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004374)  
Sir, we'll have NEA check muna the status of the co-op.

**[03:46:18.840] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004375)  
Yes, and then we'll work on it, Mr. Chair.

**[03:46:20.940] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004376)  
Yeah, Mr. Chair.

**[03:46:21.880] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004377)  
Maybe offhand, maybe I would suppose any collection or any spending should cover the operation and maintenance of the solar plant.

**[03:46:37.560] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004378)  
But we will check on it, Mr. Chair Kong, on how to go about your request.

**[03:46:42.660] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004379)  
Okay, and I would like also to check the status of the upgrading of the Luzon-Visayas transmission line.

**[03:46:51.880] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004380)  
Because malaking tulong din ito to avoid power shedding in Region 8 and probably Region 6 also.

**[03:47:00.060] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004381)  
Ito yung primary reason talaga na mayroon tayong MLD and mas higher na line rental.

**[03:47:10.620] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004382)  
So probably the problem talaga yung NGCP that medyo ma-upgrade talaga nila yung transmission line nila the soonest possible time.

**[03:47:20.940] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004383)  
But however, per plan, it will be upgraded on 2032.

**[03:47:26.200] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004384)  
Ano kaya yung possibility na it will be mas malapit pa, Madam?

**[03:47:31.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004385)  
Thank you for the last question.

**[03:47:33.680] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004386)  
Thank you for the question.

**[03:47:38.040] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004387)  
We're trying to figure out the long-term solution for this.

**[03:47:43.500] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004388)  
What we are working on today, we've finished Mindoro and Palawan.

**[03:47:48.560] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004389)  
We are doing a master plan for Visayas.

**[03:47:50.940] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004390)  
The best solution for Visayas is for each island to be independent on their energy needs.

**[03:48:00.220] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004391)  
Dapat ang base load, kung anumang kailangan nila, nandyan sa isla mismo.

**[03:48:04.100] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004392)  
Hindi yung mag-i-import kapag galing Luzon or even from Cebu.

**[03:48:08.120] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004393)  
So we are doing the analysis now.

**[03:48:11.160] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004394)  
We're just trying to balance the cost per kilowatt as to what is needed to be constructed.

**[03:48:17.500] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004395)  
It will be a long-term solution, but it is a solution, sir.

**[03:48:20.940] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004396)  
So, iniisa-isa namin yung isla.

**[03:48:24.620] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004397)  
Major islands would be five.

**[03:48:27.660] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004398)  
Panay, Negros, Cebu, Bohol, late summer.

**[03:48:31.560] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004399)  
So, we can present it to the Honorable Congressman.

**[03:48:36.800] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004400)  
Once we finish, give us one more month.

**[03:48:39.440] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004401)  
Kasi kailangan namin isimulate at simulate.

**[03:48:41.640] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004402)  
Because we cannot rely on the connection of Luzon to Visayas and Visayas to Mindanao all the time.

**[03:48:48.220] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004403)  
It should just be a reserve.

**[03:48:50.940] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004404)  
Kung kailangan, pwede tayong mag-i-import.

**[03:48:53.580] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004405)  
But I think that is the best solution for all the islands.

**[03:48:56.740] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004406)  
I mean, I'm from Panay and we pay about three pesos per four-line rental at one point.

**[03:49:02.300] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004407)  
So, kailangan talaga may independence ang kada isa.

**[03:49:06.420] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004408)  
So, we're doing the master plan.

**[03:49:08.440] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004409)  
And in due time, sir, we will present the solution.

**[03:49:10.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004410)  
Yes, can this be submitted to the committee?

**[03:49:12.880] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004411)  
Mga October, sir.

**[03:49:14.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004412)  
Yes.

**[03:49:14.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004413)  
Thank you, ma'am.

**[03:49:15.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004414)  
And I think that's the last question.

**[03:49:18.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004415)  
But may yung first question about the...

**[03:49:20.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004416)  
Island.

**[03:49:21.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004417)  
I would like to recognize si Koko.

**[03:49:25.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004418)  
President Koko, nag-release to answer.

**[03:49:27.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004419)  
Hindi ata nakasagot yun.

**[03:49:28.880] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-004420)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:49:30.380] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-004421)  
Regarding the concern of the Honorable Tan,

**[03:49:33.740] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-004422)  
the island actually has sufficient generators and is operating at eight hours at the moment.

**[03:49:39.760] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-004423)  
So, on the part of NAPO Corps, we commit to study the economics of it and try to increase it.

**[03:49:44.380] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-004424)  
The current peak demand is 108 kilowatts.

**[03:49:49.000] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-004425)  
So, pag-aralan po natin yun.

**[03:49:50.940] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-004426)  
And kung kaya po ng MOE natin, then we will expand it para umunlad lalo yung inyong isla.

**[03:49:59.080] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-004427)  
Salamat po, Mr. Chair.

**[03:49:59.840] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004428)  
Thank you, Congresswoman President Koko.

**[03:50:02.840] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004429)  
Thank you.

**[03:50:03.320] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004430)  
And I agree also with the good Secretary that each region naman, like in Region 8, we have a geothermal source of energy.

**[03:50:14.220] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004431)  
So, mas better siguro na we utilize the local source natin.

**[03:50:17.580] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004432)  
So, anyway, thank you, Madam Secretary.

**[03:50:20.480] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004433)  
Thank you.

**[03:50:20.940] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004434)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, for the time.

**[03:50:22.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004435)  
Thank you, Congressman Tan.

**[03:50:23.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-004436)  
Next, to interpolate from the majority, Congressman John Chan.

**[03:50:30.240] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004437)  
Salamat po, Mr. Chair.

**[03:50:31.660] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004438)  
And magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[03:50:33.600] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004439)  
Hello po.

**[03:50:34.500] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004440)  
So, may onting katanungan lang po ako na malapit po sa puso ko.

**[03:50:38.440] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004441)  
So, yung pinakauna po ay tungkol po sa poverty rule.

**[03:50:42.620] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004442)  
So, Madam Secretary, sa Special Provision No. 1 of the May chapter in Volume 3 of the very budget before this committee po,

**[03:50:52.440] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004443)  
naka-direct po ang NEA to prioritize yung mga sityo where, quoting the provision,

**[03:50:56.780] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004444)  
the absolute number of indigents and the incidence of poverty are high.

**[03:51:00.780] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004445)  
So, as identified in the latest official poverty statistics of the PSA.

**[03:51:06.100] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004446)  
So, yung mga statistics po, meron po tayo yung sa 2023 na full-year official poverty statistics,

**[03:51:13.520] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004447)  
which count 1,735,690 poor Filipinos and 279,220 poor families in the Bicol region.

**[03:51:22.440] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004449)  
So, so far, it's the largest absolute count of any region in the country.

**[03:51:28.520] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004450)  
So, yung tanong ko lang po, how will the NEA apply Special Provision No. 1 in the 2027 rollout, Madam Secretary?

**[03:51:38.400] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004451)  
And of the sityos program for energization next year, how many are in the regions with the highest poverty counts po?

**[03:51:45.320] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004452)  
Thank you.

**[03:51:45.780] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004453)  
So far, following that also, but also as a priority,

**[03:51:51.760] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004454)  
one of the first instructions I've given to NEA is prioritize Mindanao, actually.

**[03:51:59.280] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004455)  
It's one of the most in need, actually.

**[03:52:01.980] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004456)  
But on the side of Bicol area, Admin can further explain.

**[03:52:06.840] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004457)  
Thank you.

**[03:52:08.840] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004458)  
Tama po yun.

**[03:52:10.100] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004459)  
I was given direction.

**[03:52:12.500] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004460)  
Hinahabol po namin ang Mindanao, especially on those geographically isolated and disadvantageous areas.

**[03:52:20.560] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004461)  
And so far, majority of the funding of the SEP was dedicated for the island of Mindanao.

**[03:52:30.980] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004462)  
However, dun po sa mga, especially on Region 5, meron din naman po.

**[03:52:37.480] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004463)  
Medyo, hindi ko lang ko alam ang exact figures, but meron din nung allocation ng Kasoreko 1, 2, 3, and 4,

**[03:52:44.880] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004464)  
and even especially Aleko, Albay.

**[03:52:47.320] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004465)  
And we are trying to...

**[03:52:50.560] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004466)  
stretch the limited funding, having as a national policy, habulin ho namin ang Mindanao.

**[03:52:59.500] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004467)  
Because when I assume office, ang energization level po ng Mindanao is only at 75%.

**[03:53:06.660] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004468)  
So, yun po ang aming hinahabol.

**[03:53:10.520] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004469)  
That's why way back 2023, ang Luzon po ay nasa 95,

**[03:53:19.400] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004470)  
ang Visayas ay nasa 94, pero we ended up at 89%.

**[03:53:24.600] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004471)  
Only on the franchise areas of electric cooperatives because of Mindanao.

**[03:53:29.440] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004472)  
So, nakita naman po namin, pinuntahan po namin yung mga areas na yan, talaga pong kailangan nila ng kuryente ron.

**[03:53:35.540] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004473)  
So, wag po kayo magalala.

**[03:53:37.320] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004474)  
At lahat po yan ay ginagawa namin ng plano sa yung tinatawag naming ELTER.

**[03:53:46.440] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004475)  
Lahat po yan ay pinipili po namin talaga.

**[03:53:49.400] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004476)  
Yung talagang matindi ang pangangailangan.

**[03:53:51.780] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004477)  
Marami salamat po.

**[03:53:53.240] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004478)  
Marami salamat po.

**[03:53:55.140] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004479)  
Isa rin pong tanong po.

**[03:53:57.160] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004480)  
Actually, natuwa po ako nakita na nung start of 2026, Secretary,

**[03:54:01.980] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004481)  
nag-issue po kayo ng update dun sa ER 1-94

**[03:54:06.240] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004482)  
na in-upget po natin from 1% to 3% yung makukuha po ng mga geothermal, yung mga plants po,

**[03:54:12.880] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004483)  
within the communities who are supposed to benefit from them.

**[03:54:16.020] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004484)  
So, gusto ko lang po malaman, Secretary,

**[03:54:17.980] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004485)  
mayroon po ba tayong...

**[03:54:19.400] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004486)  
...intention or plan in the future na mas palakasin po itong pataasin?

**[03:54:26.580] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004487)  
Kasi napansin ko po tuwing umiikot po ako sa Region 5,

**[03:54:30.120] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004488)  
ang lagi po nila sinasabi ay nandito po sa atin ang mga geothermal plants,

**[03:54:34.380] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004489)  
yung mga hydropower plants, pero bakit ang taas pa rin daw po ng kuryente sa kanila.

**[03:54:54.020] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004495)

**[03:54:59.900] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004496)

**[03:55:18.060] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004500)

**[03:55:45.800] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004508)

**[04:08:26.750] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004894)  
Yun po ba?

**[04:08:27.250] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004895)  
We need, Mr. Chair,

**[04:08:29.550] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004896)  
we need to see,

**[04:08:30.990] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004897)  
the optimization, optimal plan nila muna

**[04:08:34.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004898)  
bago kami mag-decide on this

**[04:08:36.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004899)  
because we don't want to,

**[04:08:37.650] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004900)  
you cannot just rush this eh.

**[04:08:39.370] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004901)  
Kailangan talaga aral ito kasi very

**[04:08:41.470] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004902)  
technical siya.

**[04:08:43.450] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004903)  
Okay.

**[04:08:45.110] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004904)  
But yun ba yung pinakita nyo kanina?

**[04:08:47.330] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004905)  
You propose a budget of

**[04:08:49.150] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004906)  
7.5 billion

**[04:08:50.710] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004907)  
over four phases

**[04:08:53.070] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004908)  
para ma-tanggal

**[04:08:55.790] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004909)  
ang

**[04:08:56.390] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004910)  
this is a technical system loss.

**[04:08:59.350] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004911)  
Is that correct?

**[04:09:00.990] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004912)  
It's, it's for the non-technical.

**[04:09:03.110] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004913)  
For the non-technical.

**[04:09:04.410] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004914)  
I, I, I want to explain.

**[04:09:05.750] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004915)  
The pilferage should be zero right away.

**[04:09:09.290] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004916)  
Yes.

**[04:09:10.110] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004917)  
Yun yung ano namin.

**[04:09:11.130] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004918)  
Yun yung, that's our, our idea na

**[04:09:13.930] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004919)  
dapat from day one, once we issue this,

**[04:09:17.270] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004920)  
zero na siya kaagad.

**[04:09:19.050] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004921)  
May mga non-technical na minsan nami-mix siya,

**[04:09:22.970] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004922)  
kaya bantayan ng maayos ng DOE, NEA, and the electric co-op and ERC,

**[04:09:29.330] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004923)  
ah,

**[04:09:30.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004924)  
na namin-mix dyan sa non-technical,

**[04:09:32.050] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004925)  
dapat bawal yan.

**[04:09:33.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004926)  
So, this is,

**[04:09:34.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004927)  
this is for us to reinforce, no?

**[04:09:36.710] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004928)  
Ah,

**[04:09:37.550] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004929)  
now, the, the other side of it

**[04:09:39.310] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004930)  
is like, kailangan ang inefficiency

**[04:09:41.970] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004931)  
na kinukos, ang cost niya is non-technical.

**[04:09:45.090] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004932)  
Yun yung mga kailangan ayusin yung mga linya

**[04:09:47.510] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004933)  
or yung mga metro nila.

**[04:09:49.190] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004934)  
Ah, that will take some money.

**[04:09:50.490] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004935)  
Like what they did in Zamboanga.

**[04:09:52.110] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004936)  
For example, pinalitan lahat, naayos nila,

**[04:09:54.670] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004937)  
nabawasan yung, nabawasan ang non-technical loss nila.

**[04:09:58.370] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004938)  
So, that's, that's easier to implement.

**[04:10:00.050] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004939)  
This will not be an additional budget, Mr. Chair.

**[04:10:03.050] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004940)  
Ah, we will find a way how to assist the electric co-ops

**[04:10:08.050] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004941)  
without asking for, ah, from the National Fund, Mr. Chair.

**[04:10:12.050] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004942)  
Could you elaborate on that?

**[04:10:14.050] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004943)  
Kasi may pinakita kayo, 7.5 billion yung nandun,

**[04:10:18.050] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004944)  
pero sinasabi niyo hindi manggagaling sa budget.

**[04:10:20.050] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004945)  
Saan manggagaling ito?

**[04:10:21.050] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004946)  
It could come from the cooperative itself.

**[04:10:24.050] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004947)  
It could also come, because they have earnings na rin, ano?

**[04:10:27.050] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004948)  
It could come also from a loan that, ah,

**[04:10:29.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004949)  
we can facilitate.

**[04:10:30.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004950)  
Ah, we are studying, still studying the, ah, several options.

**[04:10:34.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004951)  
Ang, ang, the main point is, ah,

**[04:10:37.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004952)  
dapat ang tao hindi nagbabayad pabalik nito.

**[04:10:40.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004953)  
Yung binawas mo, hindi dapat bayaran nila ulit.

**[04:10:43.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004954)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[04:10:45.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004955)  
Ah, thank you.

**[04:10:46.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004956)  
So, pwedeng manggaling sa co-op, ah, pwedeng, sabi niyo, loan.

**[04:10:51.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004957)  
Ultimately, babayaran din yan.

**[04:10:53.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004958)  
So, yun nga ang concern.

**[04:10:55.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004959)  
Siguro, mas, ah, ah, um,

**[04:10:57.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004960)  
magsabit na lang in writing, mahirap ngayon, eh.

**[04:11:01.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004961)  
Kasi nagtataka tayo, bakit kailangan ng 7.5 billion para ma-implement?

**[04:11:06.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004962)  
Are you talking about yung mga co-op kailangan tulungan para mag-upgrade ng kanilang facilities?

**[04:11:13.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004963)  
Is that it?

**[04:11:14.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004964)  
Mr. Chair, ah, not necessarily.

**[04:11:16.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004965)  
Some co-ops can, can afford to facilitate that also.

**[04:11:20.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004966)  
But there are also a cluster of co-ops na medyo, medyo ailing na rin.

**[04:11:25.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004967)  
At baka hindi kaya.

**[04:11:26.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004968)  
At baka hindi kaya.

**[04:11:27.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004969)  
And that's where we come in also, Mr. Chair.

**[04:11:29.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004970)  
That's a case-to-case basis.

**[04:11:30.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004971)  
That's 130 co-ops na kailangan i-assess namin isa-isa, Mr. Chair.

**[04:11:36.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004972)  
Plus the, ah, the private distribution utilities.

**[04:11:39.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004973)  
Okay.

**[04:11:40.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004974)  
Thank you, Chair.

**[04:11:42.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004975)  
Another topic muna.

**[04:11:43.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004976)  
Um, so, yung repeated yellow and red alerts, lalo na sa Visayas at Mindanao.

**[04:11:50.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004977)  
Um, ano-ano po ba yung mga planta?

**[04:11:55.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004978)  
Bakit po?

**[04:11:56.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004979)  
Ano?

**[04:11:57.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004980)  
At mukhang, ah, of course, the answer there is extended maintenance.

**[04:12:01.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004981)  
Mga ganon.

**[04:12:02.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004982)  
Down yung mga planta.

**[04:12:03.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004983)  
Ano-anong mga generators ito?

**[04:12:05.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004984)  
At anong ginagawa nyo para, ah, ma-address yung situation?

**[04:12:12.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004985)  
Including possible, ah, penalties for these, ah, plants.

**[04:12:16.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004986)  
No?

**[04:12:17.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004987)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[04:12:18.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004988)  
Ah, ah, I'll, I'll have, ah, you should give her, ah, the, the two experts in the,

**[04:12:23.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004989)  
ah, on this and, ah, ah, ah, Usec, ah, Maracigan to, to address the issue.

**[04:12:28.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004990)  
This is a very, very important issue.

**[04:12:30.890] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004991)  
Yes.

**[04:12:31.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-004992)  
Yes.

**[04:12:32.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-004993)  
Usec Maracigan, you are recognized.

**[04:12:33.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-004994)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[04:12:35.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-004995)  
Well, first, let me, ah, let me present what the cases are in the Visayas, which is really

**[04:12:42.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-004996)  
the main concerns that we have at the moment.

**[04:12:44.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-004997)  
For the Visayas, ah, it so happens that the planned maintenance happens at the site of

**[04:12:51.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-004998)  
our small facilities like biomass and hydropower generations.

**[04:12:58.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-004999)  
We understand that summer, we are in the middle of the summer, so there is reduced water capacity.

**[04:13:05.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005000)  
Plus, it's an out-milling season for biomass.

**[04:13:08.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005001)  
So, reduced capacity.

**[04:13:10.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005002)  
Unfortunately, aside from the planned maintenance program of some of our facilities, there was

**[04:13:17.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005003)  
a lack of water.

**[04:13:19.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005004)  
Ah, ah, ah, ah.

**[04:13:20.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005005)  
Ah, ah, ah.

**[04:13:21.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005006)  
So, we have a major incident that happened that two major units of coal facilities in

**[04:13:26.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005007)  
the Visayas gave way one after the other, belonging to the same company.

**[04:13:32.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005008)  
And, ah, upon our discovery, the defect was on the manufacturing.

**[04:13:37.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005009)  
It was the turbine rotor that had a major concern.

**[04:13:43.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005010)  
And they had to ship out the turbine, ah, to be able to be repaired.

**[04:13:48.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005011)  
We also lacked the capacity to test the energy.

**[04:13:50.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005012)  
So, they sent it to India just for calibration.

**[04:13:51.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005013)  
So, it took a while.

**[04:13:52.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005014)  
But here, now and then, ah, since much of the facilities are being forced to run outside

**[04:14:01.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005015)  
their operating procedures, then one after the other, the other facilities gave way.

**[04:14:06.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005016)  
Now, in the last month, it so happens that this, Big Danau also has a scheduled maintenance

**[04:14:14.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005017)  
program for some of their facilities.

**[04:14:15.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005018)  
Ah, ah, ah.

**[04:14:16.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005019)  
Ah, ah, ah.

**[04:14:17.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005020)  
Ah, ah, ah.

**[04:14:18.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005021)  
Ah, ah, ah.

**[04:14:19.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005022)  
Ah, ah, ah.

**[04:14:20.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005023)  
We are now providing additional capacities to Visayas to the tune of up to 450 megawatts.

**[04:14:29.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005024)  
With the reduced capacity of the Visayas, the import from Big Danau was forced at full

**[04:14:38.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005025)  
450.

**[04:14:39.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005026)  
Even Incluson is also assisting at 250 megawatts.

**[04:14:44.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005027)  
Ah, ah, ah.

**[04:14:45.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005028)  
Ah.

**[04:14:46.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005029)  
Ah.

**[04:14:47.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005030)  
Ah.

**[04:14:48.890] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005031)  
the magnitude of the reduction in capacity.

**[04:14:51.690] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005032)  
But with the scheduled maintenance program in Mindanao,

**[04:14:55.490] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005033)  
the transfer of capacity to Visayas is also reduced.

**[04:15:00.370] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005034)  
Unfortunately, coinciding again, there were first outages also of other coal facilities in Mindanao.

**[04:15:10.870] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005035)  
For the capacity for Visayas, poor Mindanao is further reduced to almost zero.

**[04:15:17.270] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005036)  
That is the reason why currently we have red and yellow alerts for Visayas and a long yellow alerts for Mindanao.

**[04:15:26.430] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005037)  
Now, what is the DOE doing?

**[04:15:28.210] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005038)  
First, we have already done the monitoring and assessment of each and every facilities because we have now a performance department circular assessing on the compliance and non-compliance of each generation facility.

**[04:15:48.650] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005039)  
Of course, the penalty will not come from the DOE, but rather to be endorsed to the Energy Regulatory Commission.

**[04:15:56.030] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005040)  
Also, since we recognize...

**[04:15:58.210] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005041)  
The difficulties we had in the Visayas, we have identified energy solution and that is providing battery energy storage system in the Visayas.

**[04:16:10.850] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005042)  
So, we have identified up to 250 megawatts of battery energy storage that will support the auxiliary requirement for Visayas.

**[04:16:21.350] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005043)  
Right now, some 150 megawatts of this 250 are already in place.

**[04:16:28.210] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005044)  
And they are being tested at the moment.

**[04:16:30.370] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005045)  
Hopefully, it is steering its commissioning.

**[04:16:34.390] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005046)  
So, but in the meantime, we are also reviewing the possibilities of adding more auxiliary services for Visayas beyond what is required.

**[04:16:48.330] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005047)  
Particularly on the disposable requirement.

**[04:16:51.450] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005048)  
Because it happens that the requirement for Visayas...

**[04:16:58.210] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005049)  
In extended period has also affected Mindanao.

**[04:17:03.370] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005050)  
Then, to support this as a long-term solution, when as mentioned earlier by Secretary Guerin, we are doing a master plan for the Visayas, including distributed energy generation to have each and every island of Visayas the full capacity to generate for itself.

**[04:17:23.370] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005051)  
While still interconnected to provide common auxiliaries.

**[04:17:28.210] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005052)  
So, those are the plans that we have for both Visayas and Mindanao.

**[04:17:32.970] DOE Undersecretary Mario Marasigan** (asr-005053)  
Thank you.

**[04:17:34.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005054)  
Sir, this is not enough time to explain what you just said, right?

**[04:17:38.730] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005055)  
But what is clear is that, in general, particularly in Visayas, now the bioethanol is down and the coal plants have problems.

**[04:17:47.610] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005056)  
Mindanao is fast-growing, so it is already full.

**[04:17:51.090] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005057)  
Is that right?

**[04:17:52.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005058)  
Okay.

**[04:17:53.570] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005059)  
Mr. Chair.

**[04:17:55.650] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005060)  
If I can answer that.

**[04:17:56.870] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005061)  
Thank you, Gary.

**[04:17:58.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005062)  
We have a long-term solution because there are already plants that we have identified, but putting up a coal power plant is minimum three years.

**[04:18:10.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005063)  
Even gas is five years.

**[04:18:13.070] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005064)  
The fastest solution is solar, but it can't be just solar because it's intermittent.

**[04:18:19.250] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005065)  
So, batteries are our complement.

**[04:18:21.790] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005066)  
So, IRIS is integrated with solar and batteries.

**[04:18:26.290] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005067)  
That would be the fastest way.

**[04:18:28.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005068)  
So, the long-term, we have plants that will come in.

**[04:18:32.050] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005069)  
The earliest is 2028, and that will stabilize Visayas, bring in new plants with better technologies.

**[04:18:39.250] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005070)  
Now, the short-term, that's what Yusef Mario is saying, that he will put batteries of about 200 to 300 megawatts,

**[04:18:49.070] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005071)  
and that will have the yellow and red alert.

**[04:18:52.830] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005072)  
It will decrease.

**[04:18:54.090] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005073)  
But we need also the mid-term.

**[04:18:58.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005074)  
The long-term solution is that we will put in barges or ramp up our diesel, our liquid fuel, while we wait for the long-term.

**[04:19:12.070] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005075)  
Those are the solutions that we have seen, and we are already implementing.

**[04:19:16.290] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005076)  
But it will take time, really, the solution that is long-term.

**[04:19:22.990] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005077)  
Because, basically, our plants are old, and we don't have enough.

**[04:19:28.210] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005078)

**[04:26:01.860] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005153)  
Given the rise of rooftop solar adoption,

**[04:26:04.600] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005154)  
how does the ERC evaluate the impact of net-metering backfeed on technical system losses and what regulatory safeguards are in place

**[04:26:12.600] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005155)  
to prevent those associated costs from being shifted into non-solar consumers?

**[04:26:15.920] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005156)  
solar consumers.

**[04:26:21.520] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005156-split-2)  
We're well aware, Mr. Chair, of

**[04:26:42.700] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005157)  
of that possibility as soon as there's enough solar rooftop installations

**[04:26:50.460] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005158)  
that are online in the distribution utilities network right now we're considering the

**[04:26:59.020] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005159)  
possibility of expanding our distributed energy resources and including as a part of

**[04:27:08.940] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005160)  
the installations that a dr user can install would be aside from the solar rooftop would be a

**[04:27:18.700] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005161)  
battery so

**[04:27:19.740] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005162)  
somehow this will ease the impact of solar exports to the distribution utilities

**[04:27:27.980] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005163)  
but we are well aware also that under the current rate setting methodologies as there are more and

**[04:27:35.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005164)  
more solar exports to the grid there will be cannibalization of the market of the distribution

**[04:27:42.540] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005165)  
utility and there may be also need to address the rate setting setting

**[04:27:49.740] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005166)  
as well as the technical aspect of the operations of the distribution utility

**[04:27:57.020] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005167)  
because there will be more and more exports to it of of uh intermittent energy and there might be

**[04:28:09.020] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005168)  
some impact already on the on the quality of its energy that is being supplied to its other customers

**[04:28:19.740] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005169)  
we're addressing this through further studies on what the distribution utilities will need

**[04:28:28.840] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005170)  
to prepare for and what possible amendments will have to be introduced once we are done

**[04:28:37.760] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005171)  
with the 2026 Philippine Grid Code.

**[04:28:40.540] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005172)  
We intend to also revisit our current Philippine Distribution Code so we can provide for the

**[04:28:51.140] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005173)  
appropriate interconnection standards of these solar rooftops, especially those that are

**[04:28:59.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005174)  
bigger in capacity and will already be falling under the distributed energy resources category.

**[04:29:05.780] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005175)  
The lines we have designed are the following.

**[04:29:10.520] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005176)  
The line we have designed is that the current comes from the grid to the substation to the

**[04:29:19.440] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005177)  
transformer, to the consumer, one way only.

**[04:29:24.600] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005178)  
My question is, are the lines that we have drawn in this system, will this not affect

**[04:29:33.320] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005179)  
the system loss that will increase?

**[04:29:35.240] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005180)  
Our initial observation based on our discussions,

**[04:29:43.720] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005181)  
our discussions with the Paris Distribution Utilities, the changes that we saw in our

**[04:29:51.720] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005182)  
systems from the wholesale down to the distribution level

**[04:29:57.800] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005183)  
ang pinaka

**[04:30:00.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005186)  
Critical would be the installation of the metering facilities

**[04:30:09.680] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005187)  
But insofar as the impact of these developments on system loss, considering that this exported energy will be consumed in that same vicinity,

**[04:30:26.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005188)  
we foresee that the impact of the current lines that have been installed will be minimal aside from the fact that whenever there should be new solar facilities or rooftop facilities that are interconnected to the distribution grid,

**[04:30:53.200] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005189)  
one of the requirements is the conductance.

**[04:30:56.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005190)  
By the distribution utility of the distribution impact study, and that distribution impact study will be the opportunity for the distribution utility to evaluate the possible impact of that connection

**[04:31:14.500] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005191)  
and accordingly prescribe whatever will be needed to allow for that connection such as the installation of new metering facilities

**[04:31:25.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005192)  
or the distribution utility of the distribution impact study.

**[04:31:26.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005193)  
Thank you.

**[04:31:26.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005194)  
Or is there a refusal to connect, especially for those above the net metering threshold, unless certain investments will be made also by the intending customer that will connect a solar facility?

**[04:31:45.680] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005195)  
So, sa inyong buong palagay, hindi na kailangan baguhin ang sistema ng pag-generate ng kuryente.

**[04:31:58.610] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005196)  
Okay.

**[04:31:59.350] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005197)  
Sa...

**[04:31:59.970] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005198)  
Kasalukoy ang infrastruktura.

**[04:32:06.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005199)  
Nagbabago na po talaga yung kompleksyon noong pag-generate ng kuryente.

**[04:32:12.340] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005200)  
Dating centralized, highly centralized yung ating power system nang gagaling sa mga malalaking mga generation plants na nakakonekta sa transmission.

**[04:32:24.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005201)  
Later on, nakita natin na yung mga ibang planta nag-e-embed na sa distribution network.

**[04:32:30.460] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005202)  
At ngayon po ay nakikita natin na yung mga...

**[04:32:32.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005203)  
individual na mga consumers mayroon ng kapabilidad na makapag-generate din para sa sarili nilang pangangailangan at makapagbenta rin, makapag-export.

**[04:32:45.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005204)  
Kaya patuloy din po yung ginagawa nating pag-aaral kung ano ang dapat baguhin doon sa regulatory framework.

**[04:32:52.120] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005205)  
Nabanggit ko nga po yung tungkol doon sa paniningil ng distribution charge ng distribution utilities.

**[04:32:57.780] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005206)  
Maaring magkaroon nito ng impact dahil habang dumadami yung mayroong mga...

**[04:33:02.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005207)  
generation facilities, nababawasan ng kilowatt-hour sales ang benta nung ating mga distribution utilities.

**[04:33:09.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005208)  
At kaya kung ang kanilang revenue ay nakabase sa per kilowatt-hour, maaring mabawasan nito at magkakaroon sila ng mga stranded assets na kanilang pinut-up.

**[04:33:22.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005209)  
Nag-tayo sila ng mga linya ng ganitong kalalaking mga kapasidad pero dahil nagkaroon ng mga pagbabago,

**[04:33:30.180] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005210)  
kompleksyon doon sa ating...

**[04:33:32.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005211)  
energy landscape at nagkaroon ng mas maraming distributed energy resources ay kinakailangan talaga ng pagbabago doon sa regulatory framework.

**[04:33:43.780] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005212)  
Kasama na din po dyan yung pagre-rebisa bukod doon sa Philippine Green Code at yung distribution code.

**[04:33:50.160] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005213)  
Yung mga pagre-rebisa ng mga interconnection technical standards, pagkakaroon din po ng regulatory framework pagdating sa battery.

**[04:33:59.540] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005214)  
Kaya ito po ay tuloy-tuloy na pag-aaral.

**[04:34:02.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005215)  
Kaya dapat tayo na tumigil lang at manatili doon sa nakagawian nung 10, 20, 30 years ago dahil nagbabago din po talaga ang landscape.

**[04:34:13.700] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005216)  
Salamat po.

**[04:34:14.340] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005217)  
So may technical study na po ba ang DOE o ERC hingginit sa net metering sa technical losses ng isang kooperatiba o ng isang distribution utility?

**[04:34:35.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005218)  
Sa ngayon po.

**[04:34:36.720] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005219)  
Hindi po po namin na-quantify kung mayroon talagang impact dahil we operated on the assumption that since itong mga export na ito will be consumed in the same area,

**[04:34:48.320] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005220)  
datawid lang doon sa kapitbahay at hindi na masyadong magiging malaki ang dadaanan, maliit lang or if not, wala pa nga or positive pa ang impact dahil sa inbis na yung kuryente manggaling sa mas malayong lugar para supplyan.

**[04:35:06.720] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005221)  
Yung kapitbahay nung isang customer na naka net meter, yung kuryente yung ginamit niya ay nanggaling na lang doon sa kapitbahay na mayroong sobra or may export.

**[04:35:17.780] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005222)  
Kung dudumihan ko o isip ko, kung ako ay ang 10 kilowatt ang kailangan ng aking bahay, kung gawin kong 25 dahil naging distribution utility na ako.

**[04:35:45.830] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005223)  
Kung...

**[04:35:46.930] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005224)  
kakayanin po nung inyong bubong, yung kapasidad na 25 kilowatt, samantalang ang inyong demand ay talagang masiservisohan lang ng 10 kilowatt, magdidepende na po yan kung ito ay naka net meter, magdidepende po nga yun yan doon sa pricing methodology.

**[04:36:04.470] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005225)  
Ngayon, ang umiiral na pricing methodology, yung bayad sa iyo sa export mo ay equivalent doon sa average generation charge ng distribution utility.

**[04:36:14.670] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005226)  
So, kung ang...

**[04:36:15.830] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005227)  
Yung pagtatayo naman nung iyong kapasidad na 20 kilowatt, on a per kilowatt hour basis, mas mababa kaysa dyan sa pricing na lalabas based on that pricing methodology, maaring hindi siya economical na mag-put up ng kapasidad higher than your own requirement.

**[04:36:38.950] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005228)  
But, kung like nakikita natin ngayon, pataas din ang trend ng generation charge.

**[04:36:45.830] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005229)  
At pababa naman, ang pababa yung per kilowatt na cost ng pagtatayo ng solar, maaring ang makainggan yan ng mayroong magtatayo ng kapasidad higit doon sa kanilang pangangailangan.

**[04:37:01.210] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005230)  
At sa puntong ito, ang question is, mayroon pang question on the stranded contract cost ng distribution utility.

**[04:37:10.290] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005231)  
Dahil maaaring kumontrata ang distribution utility ng katamtaman lang.

**[04:37:15.830] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005232)  
Para doon sa pangangailangan ng captive customer niya, at dahil nga mayroon siyang mga customer na nagsipag tayo ng kanilang self-generation facility at mayroon pang benta, mayroon pang export,

**[04:37:28.490] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005233)  
baka mga hulugan nito na magkakaroon siya ng mga kontratang pinasok na hindi naman niya magamit dahil kailangan unahin yung export ng net metering customer.

**[04:37:38.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005234)  
So, kakailangan din talaga tingnan yung implementasyon, yung current implementation ng net metering.

**[04:37:45.830] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005235)

**[04:37:55.610] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005236)

**[04:38:13.470] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005237)

**[04:38:25.990] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005238)

**[04:39:07.970] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005242)

**[04:39:43.670] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005247)

**[04:39:45.970] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-005248)

System loss.

**[04:40:04.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005251)

**[04:40:12.460] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005252)

**[04:40:15.840] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005253)

**[04:40:22.960] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005254)

**[04:40:41.910] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005257)

**[04:40:47.170] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005258)

**[04:40:51.010] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005259)

**[04:40:55.150] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005260)

**[04:40:59.190] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005261)

**[04:42:43.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005288)  
Para po sa ERC, batay sa inyong ulat as of mid-August,

**[04:42:47.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005289)

**[04:42:54.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005290)  
Mas malaki po ito kumpara sa 5,329 complaints noong 2025,

**[04:42:59.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005291)  
at 3,220 complaints.

**[04:43:01.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005292)  
189 naman noong 2024.

**[04:43:03.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005293)

**[04:43:06.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005294)  
o 77% ng complaints ngayong taon.

**[04:43:09.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005295)  
Nangangalahati pa lang po tayo ngayong 2026

**[04:43:12.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005296)  
at tungkol po sa issue o concern na kaugnay sa serbisyo ko ng Meralco.

**[04:43:18.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005297)  
Ano na pong status ng mga complaints na ito?

**[04:43:21.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005298)

**[04:43:27.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005299)

**[04:43:31.040] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005300)  
Una po, lilinawin lang namin yung aming kategorya ng consumer complaints.

**[04:43:38.740] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005301)  
Dalawang klase po kasi ang tinuturing naming complaints.

**[04:43:42.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005302)  
One, yung informal na naisasampas sa amin sa pamamagitan ng email, walk-in, letter, o referral.

**[04:43:51.900] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005303)  
Ito pong mga ganitong nature ng complaints,

**[04:43:56.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005304)  
bago po siya talaga maging formal na complaint,

**[04:43:59.040] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005305)  
ang aming ginagawa ay namamagdala.

**[04:44:02.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005306)

**[04:44:07.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005307)  
Nang sa ganon ay kung magkakaroon ng solusyon or dispute resolution,

**[04:44:14.200] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005308)

**[04:44:18.280] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005309)  
So, ang karaniwan po na nakakarating sa amin ganitong sumbong reklamo

**[04:44:25.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005310)  
ay may kinalaman doon sa bill.

**[04:44:28.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005311)  
Meron po na nagkukwestyon doon sa kanilang natanggap.

**[04:44:32.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005312)  
At mayroon din naman po ng mga instances na lumalapit sumusulat sa amin.

**[04:44:44.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005313)  
Ang kadahilanan naman ay mayroong naging inspeksyon ang utility, ang Meralco,

**[04:44:50.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005314)  
at sila ay nakitaan daw ng badges ng mayroong problema doon sa metro.

**[04:44:58.160] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005315)  
Kaya hinatak yung metro.

**[04:45:00.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005316)  
At ngayon ay sinisingil sila ng differential billing na malaki.

**[04:45:04.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005317)  
So, sumusulat sila sa amin, pinagbibigay alam na ganito yung sitwasyon nila.

**[04:45:10.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005318)  
Pagka ganito pong instances, pinapatawag namin o sinusulatan namin ang utility upang magkomento.

**[04:45:17.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005319)  
At nakakaroon ng mga conciliation hearings or conferences between the consumer complainant and the utility representative.

**[04:45:28.180] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005320)  
Kung wala pong maging resolusyon doon sa kanila pong reklamo, amin pong ina-advise sila na gawing formal na yung complaint sa pamamagitan ng pagpa-file ng formal complaint.

**[04:45:46.120] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005321)  
This is a verified complaint, kaparehan ng mga complaint na ipina-file sa mga regular courts.

**[04:45:51.900] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005322)  
Pero ginagamitan po namin ito ng summary procedure, nang sa ganun mabilis lang din po.

**[04:45:57.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005323)  
Kapag na ito na po ay na-transform into a formal complaint, ang turing na po namin dito ay isang proceeding na nangangailangan ng pag-exercise ng quasi-judicial jurisdiction or authority ng ERC.

**[04:46:14.140] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005324)

**[04:46:25.180] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005325)  
o pamisan, nakakaroon din ng formal trial.

**[04:46:27.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005326)  
Doon po sa dami ng 5,000, [unclear]. Sa ngayon po, as of the latest, ang ongoing po na consumer complaint namin sa pagdating sa Meralco ay nasa 3,657 na.

**[04:46:51.780] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005327)

**[05:06:05.190] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005619)  
Before we adopted the online proceedings through video teleconferencing,

**[05:06:13.290] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005620)

**[05:06:15.950] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005621)  
For any rate application or any petition affecting the consumers,

**[05:06:20.730] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005622)  
we conduct at least one hearing in the locality affected.

**[05:06:23.510] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005623)

**[05:06:26.590] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005624)  
dahil po ang aming...

**[05:06:27.930] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005625)

**[05:06:34.850] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005626)

**[05:06:37.570] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005627)

**[05:06:42.670] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005628)

**[05:06:46.930] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005629)  
na magbibiyahi pa sila sa Mindanao, sa Dabao, or in the case of Visayas, sa Cebu.

**[05:06:53.570] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005630)  
Pero, noong nangyari po yung pandemic at nagkaroon na rin po ng rules,

**[05:06:57.930] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005631)  
ang ERC sa online hearings,

**[05:07:01.530] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005632)  
ngayon po ang default na hearings na namin,

**[05:07:04.050] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005633)

**[05:07:07.510] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005634)

**[05:07:10.630] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005635)

**[05:07:13.270] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005636)

**[05:07:17.650] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005637)

**[05:07:22.610] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005638)

**[05:07:23.970] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005639)

**[05:07:27.930] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005640)  
platform.

**[05:07:29.510] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005641)  
Ito na po yung default namin,

**[05:07:31.490] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005642)  
unless magkaroon nga din po ng request na magkaroon ng in-person na hearing.

**[05:07:37.270] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005643)

**[05:07:42.730] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005644)

**[05:07:45.990] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005645)

**[05:07:48.450] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-005646)  
para makapag-participate at makadulog sa atin ang ating mga consumers.

**[05:12:24.210] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005710)

**[05:12:33.430] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005711)  
This is provided under RA 9513, kung saan the renewable energy sector can avail of tax incentives under the Renewable Energy Act

**[05:12:43.030] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005712)  
through the securing of a certificate of endorsement by the DOE.

**[05:12:49.790] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005713)

**[05:12:56.150] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005714)  
This is for developers and manufacturers, fabricators and suppliers sa ilalim ng Renewable Energy Act,

**[05:13:01.890] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005715)  
whether that's for the first seven years, income tax holiday, 10 years of duty-free importation,

**[05:13:08.730] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005716)  
net operating loss carryover, NOLCO for seven taxable years, etc.

**[05:13:13.730] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005717)

**[05:13:16.010] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005718)

**[05:13:19.790] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005719)

**[05:13:23.910] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005720)

**[05:13:29.470] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005721)

**[05:13:38.070] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005722)

**[05:13:44.610] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005723)

**[05:13:46.810] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005724)

**[05:13:48.830] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005725)

**[05:13:49.790] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005726)
