Hanggat hindi niya na-fulfill po yung mga sinabi po ninyo.

**[05:14:55.670] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005738)  
Tapos, pagka nakakuha na siyang service contract, saka pa lang po...

**[05:15:00.000] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005739)  
siya mag-uumpisa ng pre-development, tapos saka po yung magko-construct, tapos saka po magpo-produce.

**[05:15:07.120] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005740)  
Saka lang po siya pwede mag-benefit dun sa mga incentives under the Renewable Energy Act.

**[05:15:13.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005741)  
Siyempre po, naiintindihan natin na may mga guidelines yung pagkuhan ng FPIC

**[05:15:18.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005742)  
and generally meron niyang range kung paano hinahandle ng mga developers, etc.

**[05:15:25.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005743)  
Pero ang punot-dulo din naman po, gusto natin mapagsilbihan systematically and through a framework level

**[05:15:35.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005744)  
na yung madi-displace ay maramdaman makita yung benepisyo.

**[05:15:41.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005745)  
Lalo na po, mas marami pong mga reports na may mga ibat-ibang mga communities,

**[05:15:47.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005746)  
whether that's renewable project or hindi, pag na-displace, usually pinapaalis lang po sila.

**[05:15:53.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005747)  
So, I'm just asking po.

**[05:15:55.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005748)  
Kung kaya pong i-consider ng DOE na kung may pag-amend ng batas para to ensure na kung anuman pong labas ng mga incentives na ito,

**[05:16:04.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005749)  
maka-benefisyo po yung ating mga indigenous communities, yung madi-displace po, if ever.

**[05:16:11.100] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005750)  
Mr. Chair?

**[05:16:12.600] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005751)  
Mr. Chair?

**[05:16:13.940] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005752)  
May we respond?

**[05:16:15.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005753)  
Yes, please.

**[05:16:17.280] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005754)  
Pag-aaralan po namin at sisiguruduhin namin na makonsider po yung mga suggestion niyo.

**[05:16:22.420] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005755)  
Thank you po.

**[05:16:23.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005756)  
Thank you po, Mr. Chair.

**[05:16:25.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005757)  
Can I?

**[05:16:25.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005758)  
Ma-clarify po.

**[05:16:29.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005759)  
Under po ba ng renewable energy projects na maaaring mabukas po ang waste to energy under the framework po ng Department of Energy right now?

**[05:16:41.040] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005760)  
May ongoing, Mr. Chair, may ongoing special auction for waste to energy ngayon.

**[05:16:47.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005761)  
Can I ask kung meron po bang existing partnerships with private institutions,

**[05:16:52.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005762)  
ang Department of Energy, or even, no?

**[05:16:55.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005763)  
Paano po nai-ensure ng Department of Energy na may gabay nila yung pag-develop at pag-aproba ng mga lumalapit na gustong magtayo ng waste to energy facilities dito?

**[05:17:07.440] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005764)  
Una po ang mga, Mr. Chair, may I?

**[05:17:10.000] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005765)  
Ang mga memorandum of agreement po namin ay doon sa mga local government units kasi kailangan po ng feedstock agreement between the private waste to energy developer at saka po yung LGU.

**[05:17:21.240] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005766)  
Kung saan man gagaling yung feedstock.

**[05:17:23.920] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005767)  
Yung pong pagkakaroon.

**[05:17:25.960] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005768)  
Kailangan po ng mga technologies na ito for waste to energy, kailangan po pumasa sa Department of Science and Technology para ikumpara sa world standards at saka sa batas po natin on Clean Air Act.

**[05:17:40.140] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005769)  
Tapos, kailangan din po silang payagan ng DNR bago po sila pwedeng magtayo ng waste to energy facility.

**[05:17:48.540] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005770)  
So, lahat po ng safeguards to make sure na hindi makakaharm sa environment at sa tao.

**[05:17:55.960] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005771)  
At sa community, kasama po yun sa waste to energy.

**[05:17:59.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005772)  
So, to my understanding po, may MOA, ang LGU, and developer. Tama po ba?

**[05:18:05.540] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-005773)  
Feedstock agreement po.

**[05:18:07.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005774)  
Feedstock agreement. Ilan pong feedstock agreements na ang meron dito sa Pilipinas on waste to energy right now?

**[05:18:13.560] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005775)  
Well, the feedstock agreement is an initiative on both sides, actually.

**[05:18:20.460] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005776)  
Not on the part of DOE, pero if you want to develop a waste to energy project,

**[05:18:25.960] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005777)  
incumbent upon you to find feedstock.

**[05:18:28.700] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005778)  
Hindi pwede na sabihin mo lang sa amin na galing waste to energy dyan, pero hindi pala sa waste.

**[05:18:34.340] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005779)  
So, it's the responsibility of the developer, actually.

**[05:18:39.060] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005780)  
As we speak, I assume that the developers that are interested in joining the auction are already looking for disagreements.

**[05:18:50.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005781)  
Looped in po ba ang DOE, Mr. Chair, kapag may ganito pong agreements na gustong pasukin,

**[05:18:55.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005782)  
and eventually, ma-fafinalize?

**[05:19:01.700] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005783)  
It has always been an issue on waste to energy, yung feedstock.

**[05:19:06.660] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005784)  
Kaya wala pa nangyayari hanggang ngayon.

**[05:19:10.140] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005785)  
And that is why, that was why the law will help also, to make it more sustainable.

**[05:19:16.900] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005786)  
But, looped in in a sense, DOE looped in in disagreements, in a sense,

**[05:19:23.940] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005787)  
it is a requirement.

**[05:19:25.960] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005788)  
It is a requirement of DOE for, for them to pursue the project.

**[05:19:28.900] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005789)  
Responsibility nila yan, Mr. Chair.

**[05:19:32.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005790)  
Mr. Chair, kung meron po tayong list of current agreements, kung meron,

**[05:19:39.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005791)  
because even though wala pa tayo on an industrial level, because wala pa pong batas,

**[05:19:44.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005792)  
we are aware that may mga ilang WTE projects sa iba't ibang bahagi ng Pilipinas, modular nga lang po.

**[05:19:51.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005793)  
So, is the DOE aware of these projects po ba? Dumadaan po ba yung information na ito?

**[05:19:55.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005794)  
Do you have?

**[05:19:55.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005795)  
Do you have records kung ilan na po ba siya sa Pilipinas?

**[05:19:57.900] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005796)  
No, we don't have any information.

**[05:20:00.140] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005797)  
We'll try to find which, whether it's through the LGUs or through DNR, we'll check if we can provide information.

**[05:20:08.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005798)  
Okay, thank you.

**[05:20:08.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005799)  
We look forward to that information, Mr. Chair.

**[05:20:11.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005800)  
But, I'd like to raise, Mr. Chair, because this is a study that was published in 2023 by PLOS Climate,

**[05:20:22.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005801)  
kung saan it was found that,

**[05:20:25.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005802)  
unfortunately, WTE incinerators in the USA emit more greenhouse gas emissions per unit of electricity produced.

**[05:20:36.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005803)  
So, there's 1,707 grams of carbon dioxide produced per kilowatt hour.

**[05:20:45.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005804)  
And it is the dirtiest among any other power source.

**[05:20:48.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005805)  
So, ang tanong ko po, Mr. Chair, ay this is included.

**[05:20:55.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005806)  
So, or classified under renewable energy.

**[05:20:58.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005807)  
Paano po natin mapangahawakan nun na pwede siyang renewable energy na may ganitong findings na dirty yung nilalabas niya

**[05:21:09.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005808)  
kumpara sa iba pang mga sources of power?

**[05:21:12.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005809)  
And ano po bang plans ng DOE, if any, to reduce the emissions of greenhouse gases in WTE facilities?

**[05:21:18.120] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005810)  
Representative, kindly wind up.

**[05:21:18.800] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005811)  
Mr. Chair, it's waste to energy, technically, because the law says so.

**[05:21:25.080] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005812)  
Under the RE law, naka-include siya.

**[05:21:28.200] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005813)  
So, that's why it's classified as waste to energy.

**[05:21:31.920] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005814)  
Now, based on the studies, it's U.S. technology.

**[05:21:35.520] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005815)  
Then, we won't allow U.S. technology to be here if it's not compliant with our requirements here.

**[05:21:42.000] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005816)  
There are technologies in other countries that are even better, like Japan, Switzerland, even Singapore has that.

**[05:21:48.880] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005817)  
Even China has that.

**[05:21:50.680] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005818)  
So, marami pong available technology.

**[05:21:52.740] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005819)  
As explained earlier,

**[05:21:54.420] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005820)  
we are one of the most stringent in emissions.

**[05:21:59.900] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005821)  
We're stricter than Switzerland, actually.

**[05:22:02.500] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005822)  
So, we will follow the law in pursuing the technology that we want to be placed here.

**[05:22:10.700] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005823)  
So, we won't do it like the other countries excited in the article na sa U.S. na technology.

**[05:22:17.920] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005824)  
Then, we'll get other options because we won't allow poor or inefficient technologies here in the Philippines.

**[05:22:23.580] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005825)  
Okay.

**[05:22:24.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005826)  
As a final manifestation,

**[05:22:26.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005827)  
given that there are documented health and environmental concerns

**[05:22:30.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005828)  
raised by residents near proposed WTE sites already actually here

**[05:22:34.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005829)  
and elsewhere in the world,

**[05:22:39.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005830)  
I request the department to reconsider the classification of waste to energy under renewable energy.

**[05:22:46.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005831)  
The response I received so far was,

**[05:22:48.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005832)  
well, we can't help it.

**[05:22:50.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005833)  
Under the law, it is there.

**[05:22:52.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005834)  
But there are studies.

**[05:22:54.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005835)  
It's beyond the one that I just cited.

**[05:22:56.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005836)  
And I can also provide it to the department for its consideration and its study

**[05:23:00.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005837)  
na mas marami pong health hazards, environmental hazards,

**[05:23:05.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005838)  
na bit-bit ang waste to energy,

**[05:23:08.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005839)  
na halos ma-defeat yung purpose, yung classifying it,

**[05:23:11.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005840)  
and yung pagtingin natin sa pag-invest in renewable energy

**[05:23:14.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005841)  
na gusto naman po natin para po sa dulo ng ating framework.

**[05:23:19.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005842)  
So, that's the consideration po.

**[05:23:20.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005843)  
Sana na maibukas na sa department.

**[05:23:22.840] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005844)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[05:23:23.780] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005845)  
We cannot, unfortunately, we cannot reclassify it.

**[05:23:26.440] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005846)  
We don't have the mandate.

**[05:23:28.000] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005847)  
But should the good, honorable congresswoman propose it,

**[05:23:32.580] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005848)  
then we can join the discussion and the study.

**[05:23:35.360] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005849)  
Yes.

**[05:23:35.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005850)  
My time is up, Mr. Chair.

**[05:23:37.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005851)  
Thank you very much.

**[05:23:38.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005852)  
Who said your time is up?

**[05:23:40.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005853)  
The timer said my time is up.

**[05:23:42.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005854)  
Okay.

**[05:23:42.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005855)  
But if...

**[05:23:44.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005856)  
For the second round.

**[05:23:45.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005857)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[05:23:46.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005858)  
Thank you.

**[05:23:52.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005861)

**[05:24:00.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005866)

**[05:24:08.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005868)

**[05:24:09.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005869)

**[05:24:10.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005870)

Supply of diesel and fuel.

The prices.

**[05:24:41.180] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005878)  
and the entire Filipino people.

**[05:24:43.820] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005879)

**[05:24:47.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005880)  
together with my brother, the more handsome one,

**[05:24:51.660] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005881)  
the congressman of Abante Mindanao,

Party List.

Nothing of the fears became realities.

Thanks to Secretary Sharon Garin.

**[05:25:16.500] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005890)

**[05:25:17.500] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005891)  
We were able to contain the increases of fuel prices.

**[05:25:23.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005892)  
Through her, we were able to be able to provide fuel prices.

**[05:25:25.000] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005893)  
Through her, we were able to be able to provide fuel prices.

**[05:25:25.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005894)  
Through her, we were able to be able to provide fuel prices.

**[05:25:25.900] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005895)  
Through her, we were able to provide fuel prices.

**[05:25:26.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005896)  
Through her, we were able to be able to gather government resources

**[05:25:28.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005897)  
Through her, we were able to be able to be able to be able to be able to give to the transport sector

**[05:25:30.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005898)  
Through her, we were able to be able to be able to be able to give to the transport sector

**[05:25:34.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005899)  
and the Filipino people.

**[05:25:36.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005900)  
That is why the Congressman Maxi Rodriguez, Jr.

**[05:25:40.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005901)  
and your representation here

**[05:25:42.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005902)  
and your representation here

**[05:25:44.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005903)  
are here not only to support the budget to the DOE

**[05:25:47.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005904)  
but to increase the budget of the Department of Energy.

**[05:25:52.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005905)

**[05:25:55.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005906)  
are asking? None.

**[05:25:58.200] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005907)  
Mr. Chair, we don't

**[05:25:59.680] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005908)

**[05:26:01.220] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005909)

Kailangan talaga

**[05:26:05.380] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005911)  
we have a strong regulator.

**[05:26:07.540] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-005912)  
Yes, it's just

**[05:26:08.840] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005913)  
220 million.

non-private…

**[05:47:18.700] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006287)  
Yan ang pinakamahalaga sa akin.

**[05:47:21.200] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006288)

**[05:47:23.380] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006289)

**[05:47:25.260] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006290)

There are several components, electric vehicles, more exploration, electrification of our appliances.

**[05:47:55.260] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006291)  
Lahat-lahat po, we're working on that. And in addition to that, we need reserves in the country. Unfortunately, our storage facilities are private. Ginagamit lang natin yung binibilang natin. When we tell you in the news there's 50 days, it's in the private facilities.

**[05:48:13.300] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006292)  
PNOC, headed by President Chico Alvarez, is setting up, and hopefully within this term, we can set up 1 billion barrels.

**[05:48:23.220] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006293)

**[05:48:25.260] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006294)  
The storage facility na government-owned. Para over time, we increase and increase. It is true that we are protected if we have reserves. Kasi I think it was Yusek Sandy who told me that having reserves not only protects you from the unpredictability or volatility of whatever war there is in the rest of the world, it also shields us, it affects our growth.

**[05:48:55.260] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006295)  
Kasi nasi-shield tayo sa currency exchange, sa volatility of the price. So malaki talaga yung advantage sa atin pag meron tayo. So we're working on it already.

**[05:53:47.620] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006376)

**[05:53:51.840] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006377)

**[05:54:00.940] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006378)  
Ano po ang mangyayari doon sa stranded cost ng mga...

**[05:54:04.600] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006379)  
Na mga distribution utilities.

**[05:54:08.820] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006380)

**[05:54:14.800] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006381)

**[05:54:22.240] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006382)  
Hindi pa po pwedeng sabihin natin sa ERC, Madam Secretary, na i-review po yung mga ano.

**[05:54:29.280] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006383)  
At pwede po nilang ano...

**[05:54:31.300] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006384)  
Can I please ask Chairman Juan that?

**[05:54:33.860] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006385)  
Mr. Chairman?

**[05:54:35.200] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006386)  
Posible kaya yun na pag-review nyo, tanggayin nyo po yung stranded cost ng mga distribution utilities.

**[05:54:48.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006387)  
Kindly use the other mic now, siguro, if it's not functioning.

**[05:54:52.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006388)  
Pakiayos yung mic

**[05:55:02.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006389)  
...

**[05:55:02.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006390)  
Yeah, let's...

**[05:55:03.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006391)  
Siguro siya sabi lang nung ano, be concise and brief sa answer mo.

**[05:55:07.060] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006392)  
The situation will be akin to the current implementation of the ARCOA,

**[05:55:13.120] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006393)  
where we have actually advised our distribution...

**[05:55:16.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006394)  
utilities entering power supply contracts

**[05:55:18.800] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006395)  
to include in their power supply agreements a carve-out provision.

**[05:55:22.960] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006396)  
Also, if there are no carve-out provisions,

**[05:55:25.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006397)  
and the distribution utilities will be left with excess contracted capacities,

**[05:55:31.840] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006398)  
if the distribution utilities will be able to unload or renegotiate with their suppliers

**[05:55:38.400] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006399)  
so that they can have a lowering of the contracted volume,

**[05:55:42.560] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006400)  
or unload these excess contracted volumes,

**[05:55:46.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006401)  
to an affiliate RES,

**[05:55:48.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006402)  
so that it will be the RES that will be selling this contracted capacity,

**[05:55:55.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006403)  
or for these distribution utilities to participate in WESM

**[05:56:00.920] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006404)  
by offloading these excess contracted capacities in the WESM.

**[05:56:07.120] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006405)  
But, Chairman, pag hindi po pumayag yung providers,

**[05:56:11.680] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006406)  
wala na po pang recourse ang distribution utilities?

**[05:56:16.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006407)  
Because, Sir, ang issue dito, Chairman, is

**[05:56:27.540] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006408)  
we always hear the words in the hearings,

**[05:56:32.340] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006409)  
non-impairment, non-impairment.

**[05:56:34.300] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006410)  
But, pag hindi po natin gagawin yung police power,

**[05:56:37.580] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006411)  
hindi po natin gagamitin yun, the police power of the state,

**[05:56:41.100] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006412)  
to lift and to suspend these so-called non-impairment of contracts,

**[05:56:46.760] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006413)  
then, yung stranded cost na po yun,

**[05:56:50.480] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006414)  
i-charge rin po sa lahat ng consumers na maiiwi,

**[05:56:53.520] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006415)  
So, in the one hand, pinopromote po natin sila mag-solar power.

**[05:56:59.260] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006416)  
Pagkatapos, yung stranded cost,

**[05:57:02.080] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006417)  
maiiwan doon sa mga consumers that do not have the ability

**[05:57:06.280] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006418)  
to procure and to buy their solar power systems.

**[05:57:09.300] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006419)  
Mataas pa rin yung singil sa kanila.

**[05:57:11.780] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006420)  
At ang sabi po, eh, non-impairment po eh.

**[05:57:14.520] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006421)  
But, if we will continue to reason that way,

**[05:57:17.960] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006422)  
then the effects, the positive effects of disruptive technologies

**[05:57:21.640] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006423)  
will never be, will never be, will never be,

**[05:57:23.480] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006424)  
will never be, will never be, will never be,

**[05:57:23.500] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006425)  
will never be enjoyed by the people.

**[05:57:25.960] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006426)  
We will look into that, Mr. Chair.

**[05:57:31.060] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006427)  
Although, for PSAs that were contracted after the CSP policy of the DOE

**[05:57:38.020] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006428)  
and after we issued the CSP rules in 2023,

**[05:57:42.400] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006429)  
we've already prescribed a template for a PSA

**[05:57:45.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006430)  
where there's an automatic carve-out provision.

**[05:57:48.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006431)  
So, kung ang magiging stranded capacities na lang

**[05:57:53.340] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006432)  
ay yung mga hindi na-cover nito mga bagong PSAs,

**[05:57:56.020] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006433)  
na walang carve-out,

**[05:57:57.620] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006434)  
we will look into how we will be able to assist the distribution utilities

**[05:58:05.160] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006435)  
para ma-offload nila itong kontratang ito

**[05:58:09.280] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006436)  
at hindi naman ma-apekto ang remaining captive customers niya.

**[05:58:13.840] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006437)  
Thank you, Chairman.

**[05:58:15.500] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006438)  
And you may want to refer to the Supreme Court case,

**[05:58:19.780] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006439)  
yung NDC versus Philippine Veterans Bank

**[05:58:21.940] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006440)  
because narecognize po doon na yung sinasabing sanctity

**[05:58:25.960] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006441)  
of non-impairment,

**[05:58:27.040] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006442)  
pwede po natin i-override yun.

**[05:58:30.140] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006443)  
If the impact is so embedded in public welfare,

**[05:58:38.560] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006444)  
pwede po natin gawin yun.

**[05:58:41.600] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006445)  
Yung issue rin po sa systems loss,

**[05:58:46.060] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006446)  
napag-usapan po natin,

**[05:58:47.420] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006447)  
sabi po ni Madam Secretary at least,

**[05:58:49.780] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006448)  
yung mga non-technical systems loss will automatically be,

**[05:58:54.220] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006449)  
ah,

**[05:58:55.960] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006450)  
will solve that problem within one year po.

**[05:58:59.200] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006451)  
Pero,

**[05:59:00.400] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006452)  
may existing policy po yung National Electrification Administration

**[05:59:04.660] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006453)  
na yung mga distribution utilities are required doon sa sityo electrification

**[05:59:11.760] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006454)  
that they have to keep on increasing

**[05:59:14.460] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006455)  
yung pag-electrify sa mga sityos.

**[05:59:19.640] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006456)  
Posible kaya,

**[05:59:20.840] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006457)  
tanong ko sa Department of Energy ito

**[05:59:23.020] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006458)  
or sa NEA,

**[05:59:25.140] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006459)  
pwede kayang ma-suspend yun?

**[05:59:27.880] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006460)  
Dahil,

**[05:59:29.100] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006461)  
kung yung mga distribution utilities,

**[05:59:32.060] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006462)  
ipapagpatuloy po natin yung challenge sa kanila,

**[05:59:35.260] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006463)  
yung pressure na you keep on electrifying all of those sityos,

**[05:59:39.920] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006464)  
aakyat rin po yung technical systems losses.

**[05:59:43.500] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006465)  
So,

**[05:59:44.240] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006466)  
yung sinasabing yung bababa yung non-technical,

**[05:59:46.940] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006467)  
pero aakyat naman yung technical

**[05:59:48.380] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006468)  
dahil puro tayo patayin ang patayin ng sityo electrification,

**[05:59:53.520] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006469)  
eh di,

**[05:59:54.260] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006470)  
the right hand,

**[05:59:55.140] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006471)  
is taking away what the left hand is giving out.

**[06:00:00.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006472)  
Yes, sir.

**[06:01:22.660] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006485)

**[06:01:41.480] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006489)

**[06:01:44.340] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006490)

**[06:01:50.480] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006491)

**[06:01:54.060] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006492)

**[06:01:58.620] Rep. Arthur C. Yap** (asr-006494)

**[06:02:01.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006495)

**[06:02:03.780] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006496)  
Opo, yung pong pinagkukuna ng NGCP na ancillary services,

**[06:02:09.180] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006497)  
dalawa po kasi yun, through ASPA o yung kontrata or through the reserve market.

**[06:02:14.180] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006498)  
Yung through ASPA, ito po ay dumadaan sa CSP and sa approval ng ERC.

**[06:02:20.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006499)  
So, stable yung presyo dyan pagdating sa mga contracted volumes.

**[06:02:26.620] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006500)  
Ang hindi matiyak ay yung presyohan sa reserve market dahil this is market-based

**[06:02:33.560] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006501)  
and ang existing offer cap dyan sa reserve market ay 25 pesos per kilowatt hour.

**[06:02:42.120] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006502)  
At ang nakikita natin, meron nga po kaming pending na proposal sa ERC

**[06:02:48.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006503)  
dahil nakikita rin natin na parang napakataas yung lumalabas ng presyo sa reserve market

**[06:02:53.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006504)  
at initially ang pinag-propose nga namin ay,

**[06:02:56.620] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006505)  
iba ba ito to 9 pesos from 25 to 9 pesos.

**[06:03:00.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006506)  
So, pinag-aaralan na lang namin yung mga sinubmit na comments and yung mga inputs

**[06:03:05.800] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006507)  
during the public consultation and we will soon decide on this.

**[06:03:09.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006508)  
And hopefully, kung magkakaroon na ng ibang offer cap na which will be lower than the existing,

**[06:03:16.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006509)  
it will help temper yung pricing in the reserve market.

**[06:03:19.940] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006510)  
And at the same time, it will also signify na yung mga ibang may,

**[06:03:25.560] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006511)  
may capacity na pwedeng pang ancillary ay i-offer na lang nila ito sa NGCP

**[06:03:32.120] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006512)  
under the contract markets through the ASPA

**[06:03:35.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-006513)  
and mag-participate sila sa mga CSP yung ipapatawag ng NGCP.

**[06:29:44.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006864)

**[06:29:47.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006866)

**[06:29:48.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006867)

**[06:29:50.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006868)

**[06:29:52.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006869)

**[06:29:57.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006870)

**[06:29:58.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006872)

**[06:30:00.580] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006887)  
Mr. Chair, when we purchased diesel na ginagamit ng Napocor ang diesel sa mga isla niya, they were affected. They would be directly affected. Iaakit yung presyo doon ng electricity.

**[06:30:18.940] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006888)  
So, as a directive, I gave PNOC the authority to sell it to Napocor for generation of electricity at a preferential rate para hindi naman mag-suffer yung mga taga-isla natin.

**[06:30:36.900] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006889)  
And that worked with them for a while. I think about, siguro kung mga 80 yung presyo, 60 pesos ba yung binigay.

**[06:30:47.640] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006890)  
At a loss naman na binigay.

**[06:30:48.940] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006891)  
Binenta ng PNOC. But I think that's a sacrifice that we were willing to do para hindi naman mag-suffer yung mga consumers ng isla.

**[06:30:58.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006892)  
So, actually, itong magkatabi pala yung nag-deal. PNOC to Napocor, correct?

**[06:31:06.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006893)

**[06:31:08.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006894)

**[06:31:20.250] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006895)

**[06:31:42.290] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-006897)

**[06:31:50.250] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-006898)

**[06:31:55.410] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-006900)

**[06:32:00.910] NAPOCOR President and CEO Jericho Jonas B. Nograles** (asr-006902)

27 million liters?

**[06:32:12.310] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006904)

**[06:32:12.910] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006905)

**[06:32:39.170] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006911)

**[06:33:11.070] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006915)

**[06:33:17.290] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006916)

**[06:33:58.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006921)

**[06:34:01.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006922)

**[06:35:10.780] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006935)

**[06:35:13.180] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006936)

**[06:35:24.580] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006939)

**[06:35:32.100] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006940)

**[06:35:39.140] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006942)

**[06:35:42.360] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006943)

**[06:35:44.560] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006944)

**[06:35:48.380] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006945)

**[06:35:49.780] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006946)

**[06:35:53.120] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006947)  
So, kumpleto na po yan.

**[06:35:54.300] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006948)

**[06:36:00.220] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006949)

**[06:36:08.000] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006950)

**[06:36:10.820] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006951)

In your mission.

**[06:36:18.380] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006954)

**[06:36:20.220] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006955)  
In year two.

**[06:36:21.760] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006956)  
So, papunta na tayo sa...

**[06:36:23.280] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006957)

**[06:36:26.300] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006958)

**[06:36:34.040] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006959)

**[06:36:38.140] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006960)

**[06:36:44.540] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006961)

**[06:36:47.180] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006962)

**[06:36:48.380] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006963)

**[06:36:59.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006965)

**[06:37:07.100] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006967)

**[06:37:10.560] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006968)

**[06:37:18.380] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006971)

**[06:37:21.120] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006972)  
Malaki pa rin.

**[06:37:22.040] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006973)  
Billion.

**[06:37:22.660] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006974)  
Billion dollars.

**[06:37:24.040] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006975)

**[06:37:26.480] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006976)

**[06:37:30.720] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006977)

**[06:37:36.420] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006978)

**[06:37:37.240] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006979)

**[06:37:43.420] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006980)  
Sa funding, mostly.

Yung 10 billion and 4 billion.

**[06:37:51.420] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006983)  
No, no, no.

**[06:37:53.160] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006984)

**[06:37:55.060] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006985)

**[06:37:57.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006986)

**[06:38:06.680] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006989)

**[06:38:18.380] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-006991)  
France, those are the main players right now.

**[06:38:21.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006992)

**[06:56:29.340] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007264)  
With regard sa West Philippine Sea, actually, sa DOE, ang tawag natin dyan,

**[06:56:36.220] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007265)  
Northwest, offshore Northwest Palawan.

**[06:56:39.300] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007266)  
Para mas...

**[06:56:40.380] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007267)  
Mas malapit sa atin.

**[06:56:42.720] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007268)  
Offshore Northwest.

**[06:56:44.040] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007269)  
Pero in particular, yung tinatawag po nating Recto Bank o RID Bank, okay?

**[06:56:49.500] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007270)  
May service contract po ngayon dyan.

**[06:56:52.400] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007271)  
And normally, ang commercial, the way its partnership works is,

**[06:56:58.720] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007272)  
may service contractor nakikipag-farm in yung potential partner dyan.

**[06:57:04.480] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007273)  
Ang primary law dyan is that any exploration...

**[06:57:12.010] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007274)  
ng oil and gas, kailangan may service contract from the government.

**[06:57:17.690] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007275)  
And I think, yun lang ating single basis for having any joint activity.

**[06:57:25.050] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-007276)  
It has to fall under our service contract system.

**[06:57:28.490] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007277)
